### [1] [PRISM: Evaluating POSIX Storage Systems for AI Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21746)
*Adithya Kumar,Aditya Basu,Jacob Kahn,Parth Malani,Leo Huang,Kalyan Saladi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了PRISM评估框架，用于在GPU集群上评估POSIX存储系统的可用性和性能，通过模拟真实AI研究工作负载发现闪存支持的NFS方案在分布式检查点加载场景下比Lustre性能提升高达3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究中存储系统对研究效率至关重要但常被忽视；现有基准测试仅评估峰值性能，无法反映真实AI研究中突发、异构的IO模式和对POSIX兼容性、研究友好接口的需求。

Method: 引入PRISM评估框架，通过重现代表性AI研究工作负载（包括数据摄入、检查点IO和开发者工作流），在GPU集群上对POSIX存储系统进行可用性和性能维度的综合评估。

Result: 使用PRISM比较了基于Lustre和NFS的POSIX存储系统，发现在分布式检查点加载用例中，闪存支持的NFS方案比闪存支持的Lustre方案性能提升高达3倍。

Conclusion: PRISM框架能够有效评估和甄选适合不同环境的存储解决方案，帮助做出明智的集群设计决策，弥补了现有基准测试在AI研究工作负载评估方面的不足。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI research is driven by massive investments in GPU clusters, yet the critical role of storage systems in enabling efficient research workflows is often overlooked. Unlike traditional HPC workloads, AI research prioritizes researcher productivity and ease of iteration. Practitioners rely on POSIX compliant file systems for seamless prototyping, debugging, and experimentation before scaling to specialized storage backends. The primary selection criterion is therefore not peak throughput alone, but rather performance within a POSIX compatible, researcher friendly interface. However, existing benchmarks evaluate storage systems exclusively on peak performance and fail to capture the bursty, heterogeneous IO patterns characteristic of real world AI research - where workflows are dynamic, continuously evolving spanning all stages of research. We introduce PRISM, an evaluation framework that reproduces representative AI research workloads - spanning data ingestion, checkpoint IO, and developer workflows to assess and qualify POSIX storage systems along both usability and performance dimensions on GPU clusters. Using PRISM we were able to compare Lustre and NFS based POSIX storage systems across multiple research workload dimensions and select the appropriate storage solution for different environments. As a specific case study in our environment we observed that a flash backed NFS solution outperformed the flash backed Lustre solution by up to 3x for the distributed checkpoint load usecase which helped us make an informed cluster design

</details>


### [2] [Unified Static-Dynamic Pruning for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21985)
*Jinhyeok Kim,Yejoon Lee,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPDP框架，首次统一了非结构化静态剪枝和输入自适应动态剪枝，在GPU上实现了高效的大语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的部署面临自回归解码的计算和内存瓶颈问题，现有的静态剪枝缺乏适应性，而动态剪枝引入了运行时不规则性，亟需一种统一的框架来平衡效率与适应性。

Method: 设计了Tiled-CBC压缩格式和两个GPU内核：(1)带有混合激活感知动态共享内存位图解码的CUDA核spMspV内核，用于细粒度的运行时激活跳过；(2)针对预填充计算优化的张量核心SpMM内核。这种联合的格式-内核设计协调了静态和动态稀疏性。

Result: 在推理优化GPU上的综合评估显示，SPDP相比SpInfer等先进稀疏框架实现了1.24x-1.37x的平均加速（最高达2.51x），同时在困惑度匹配的情况下实现了高达25%更高的稀疏度。

Conclusion: SPDP推进了推理效率-质量的帕累托前沿，证明了统一的静态-动态剪枝可以在大规模LLM服务中带来显著的吞吐量和能效提升。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of large language models (LLMs) has magnified the computational and memory bottlenecks of autoregressive decoding, where low compute intensity and bandwidth-bound kernels dominate inference cost. Weight pruning offers a promising remedy,
  but existing methods remain confined to either static pruning (SP), which permanently removes redundant weights but lacks adaptivity, or dynamic pruning (DP), which adapts to input sparsity but introduces runtime irregularity. This paper presents SPDP, a unified
  sparse-inference framework that integrates unstructured SP with input-adaptive DP for efficient LLM inference on GPUs. SPDP co-designs a new Tiled-Column-wise Bitmap Compressed (Tiled-CBC) format and two complementary GPU kernels: (1) a CUDA-core spMspV kernel
  featuring Hybrid Activation-aware Dynamic Shared-Memory Bitmap Decoding (HAD-SMBD) for fine-grained, runtime activation skipping, and (2) a Tensor-Core SpMM kernel optimized for prefill computation. This joint format-kernel design harmonizes static and dynamic
  sparsity, maintaining bandwidth-efficient memory access and high compute intensity under both phases of LLM inference. Comprehensive evaluations on inference-optimized GPUs demonstrate that SPDP achieves 1.24x-1.37x average speedup (up to 2.51x) over state-of-the-
  art sparse frameworks such as SpInfer, while matching. perplexity with up to 25% higher sparsity. SPDP advances the inference efficiency-quality Pareto frontier, showing that unified static-dynamic pruning can deliver substantial throughput and performance-per-watt
  improvements in large-scale LLM serving

</details>


### [3] [Smart Contract Tells: Aircraft Maintenance Records Are Now Trustworthy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21989)
*Woosuk Choi,Seungmo Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于区块链的去中心化应用(dApp)，通过双层架构解决飞机维修记录分散和验证瓶颈问题，实验显示Gas消耗减少93.9%，审计成本降低90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机维修记录对适航性和资产估值至关重要，但目前记录分散在多方利益相关者之间，造成验证瓶颈和信息不对称，可能抑制飞机剩余价值。需要一种既能保证数据完整性和可追溯性，又能降低存储成本和审计时间的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于双层架构的区块链锚定去中心化应用(dApp)：1) 采用IPFS链下存储文件；2) 链上锚定内容标识符(CID)和关键元数据；3) 遵循FAA电子记录保存指导原则设计元数据和可追溯性控制；4) 链上负载保持O(1)复杂度，相比直接链上存储的O(n)显著降低。

Result: 在BNB智能链测试网上的7KB实验中：1) CID锚定相比直接链上存储减少Gas消耗93.9%；2) 审计成本模型显示在明确场景假设下潜在节省超过90%；3) 验证了原型的技术可行性和经济潜力。

Conclusion: 研究证明了所提出的区块链架构在技术上可行且具有显著的经济效益潜力，包括降低Gas消耗和审计成本，但实际财务收益仍需通过运营数据进一步验证。为飞机维修记录管理提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Aircraft maintenance records are critical to airworthiness and asset valuation, yet they are often fragmented across stakeholders, creating verification bottlenecks and information asymmetry that may suppress aircraft residual value. This paper proposes a blockchain-anchored decentralized application (dApp) based on a dual-layer architecture that combines InterPlanetary File System (IPFS)-based off-chain storage with on-chain anchoring of Content Identifiers (CIDs) and selected metadata. With respect to off-chain file size, the on-chain payload per record remains $\mathcal{O}(1)$, compared with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ for direct on-chain file storage. The architecture incorporates metadata and traceability controls informed by Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) electronic recordkeeping guidance. The main contribution is an economic framework that models the relationship between tamper-evident maintenance-record provenance, audit workflow duration, aircraft residual value, and operational cost. In a 7-kB experiment conducted on the BNB Smart Chain testnet, CID anchoring reduced gas consumption by 93.9\% compared with direct on-chain storage. Under explicitly stated scenario assumptions, the audit-cost model indicates potential savings of more than 90\%. These results support the technical feasibility of the prototype and illustrate its economic potential, while the estimated financial benefits remain to be validated using operational data.

</details>


### [4] [Accountable Transaction Inclusion Lists: Enhancing Ethereum's Censorship Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22040)
*Patrick Spiesberger,Hannes Hartenstein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FairFIL机制，通过要求构建者公开披露审查的交易并由后续构建者强制包含，将多区块审查成本提高一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以太坊审查抵抗机制（如FOCIL）缺乏问责制，委员会成员不对实际交易包含负责，攻击者可用低成本（每区块不到2欧元）贿赂整个委员会来审查交易，威胁时间敏感型应用。

Method: 提出FairFIL机制：要求构建者公开披露所有被审查交易（遵循协议锚定策略），委员会验证披露的完整性和有效性；后续构建者必须包含这些交易，遗漏将失去完整区块奖励。

Result: 实证评估表明，在以太坊主网上，FairFIL使多区块审查成本比现有提案高一个数量级，同时基本保留构建者的MEV提取自由度；内存池一致性要求在实践中可满足。

Conclusion: 问责制机制能显著提高审查成本，FairFIL在保持合理激励兼容性的同时有效增强以太坊的审查抵抗能力。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction inclusion is rarely in question; what matters is the delay until inclusion. Currently, block builders could exercise censorship across consecutive blocks, threatening time-critical applications, such as on-chain auctions. To mitigate this risk, existing proposals such as FOCIL, scheduled for deployment in late 2026, assign a committee to list transactions for mandatory inclusion. However, no committee member is held accountable for the actual inclusion of the transactions: an adversary can bribe the entire committee to omit any transaction for less than 2 Euro per block under current conditions. We argue that accountability, i.e., requiring all exclusion decisions to be publicly disclosed and verifiably complete, with violations attributable to a specific party, substantially raises censorship costs. To this end, we propose Fair Forward Inclusion Lists (FairFIL) as an accountable censorship resistance mechanism for Ethereum. In FairFIL, every builder must publish all transactions the builder chooses to censor, subject to a protocol-anchored policy; a committee verifies the completeness and validity of this disclosure. The subsequent builder must include these transactions, forfeiting the full block reward upon any omission. Therefore, under FairFIL, extending censorship beyond a single slot requires an assembler to forfeit a full block reward. We show that compliance is rational for all participants within our behavior model. Our empirical evaluation on Ethereum mainnet indicates that multi-block censorship costs one order of magnitude more than under existing proposals, while leaving the builder's MEV extraction freedom largely intact. Initial measurements further suggest that the mempool consistency FairFIL requires is met in practice.

</details>


### [5] [Duet: Co-Optimizing P2P Message Propagation and Rotating-Leader Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22209)
*Yifeng Ye,Rongji Huang,Gerui Wang,Mingchao Wan,Yuxing Duan,Jingjing Zhang,Shengyun Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区块链P2P网络优化方法，通过状态机抽象记录网络拓扑和延迟，在Tendermint和libp2p上实现了三项改进，使峰值吞吐量提升了7.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链系统中共识层与底层P2P网络相互透明，缺乏协同优化，错失了进一步提升性能的机会。区块链天然具有状态机特性，可以利用这一抽象来以可信且协调的方式记录网络拓扑和延迟信息。

Method: 提出三项改进：(1)加速领导者轮换；(2)引入可靠广播范式，正常情况下采用基于树的传播，必要时回退到gossip；(3)构建延迟感知的传播树。将这些方法集成到Tendermint共识协议和libp2p网络栈中。

Result: 在Amazon EC2平台上使用分布在10个区域的300个节点进行实证评估，与相同拓扑上的gossip传播相比，原型系统将峰值吞吐量提升了7.26倍。

Conclusion: 通过打破共识层与P2P网络之间的透明壁垒，利用区块链状态机抽象实现协调优化，可以显著提升区块链系统的消息传递效率和整体性能。

Abstract: In blockchain systems, peer-to-peer (P2P) overlay networks play a crucial role in providing reliable, scalable and efficient message-delivery services to upper layers. However, the consensus layer and the underlying P2P network remain mutually opaque in existing blockchains, waiving the opportunity for further improvement. In contrast to other P2P applications, blockchain can naturally be abstracted as a state machine. We therefore leverage this abstraction to record network topologies and latencies in a trusted and coordinated manner. With this support, we propose three improvements to rotating-leader consensus protocols and their underlying P2P networks: (1) accelerating leader rotation; (2) introducing a reliable-broadcast paradigm that employs tree-based dissemination in the normal case and falls back to gossip only when necessary; and (3) constructing latency-aware dissemination trees. We integrate the above ideas into Tendermint and libp2p, and conduct empirical evaluation on Amazon EC2 platform using up to 300 nodes distributed across 10 regions. The results demonstrate that, compared with gossip-based dissemination over the same topology, our prototype improves peak throughput by up to $7.26\times$.

</details>


### [6] [Agentic CPU-GPU Scheduling for Heterogeneous AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22242)
*Tianxi Lu,Sherief Reda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对现有AI系统默认将所有GPU兼容工具分配到GPU导致的性能次优问题，提出了一种结合LLM智能体与算法运行时监视器的调度器，在13个场景中均达到最优映射，无需离线训练且优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架默认将所有GPU兼容工具分配到GPU执行，但通过对19个AI工具的性能分析发现，仅有11个适合GPU，4个表现模糊，1个因PCIe传输开销更适合CPU，3个设备无关，证明了全GPU优先调度策略的次优性。

Method: 将设备调度建模为在VRAM预算约束下为每个工具选择即时GPU执行、排队GPU执行或CPU卸载三种方案；设计了结合LLM智能体与算法运行时监视器的调度系统，监视器通过运行平均值、对称重探测、交换重探测和探索提示扩展LLM的观察能力，但不预设具体映射方案。

Result: 在涵盖串行执行、并行竞争和内存约束执行的13个场景中，所提调度器在全部场景中均达到暴力搜索最优映射，在映射准确率上匹配最佳经典基线，同时避免了完整映射上的bandit式探索，优于HEFT、StarPU和全GPU策略且无需离线训练。

Conclusion: 证明了智能体调度器能够有效解决异构AI工具在共享GPU/CPU基础设施上的设备分配问题，实现了零离线训练下的最优性能，为Agentic AI系统的资源调度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems compose heterogeneous tool workloads on shared GPU/CPU infrastructure, yet existing frameworks assign all GPU-capable tools to the GPU by default. We profile 19 AI tools across GPU and CPU and find that 11 are GPU-preferred, 4 are ambiguous, 1 is CPU-preferred due to PCIe transfer dominance, and 3 are device-neutral, establishing that blanket GPU-first scheduling is suboptimal. We formulate device scheduling as assigning each tool to one of three options: immediate GPU execution, queued GPU execution, or CPU offload, under a VRAM budget, and identify two runtime factors that cause end-to-end latency to diverge from static profiles: GPU utilization contention and VRAM capacity contention. We present an agentic scheduler that pairs an LLM agent with an algorithmic runtime monitor, where the monitor expands what the LLM can observe via running averages, symmetric reprobing, swap reprobing, and exploration hints, without ever prescribing which mapping to adopt. Across 13 scenarios spanning serial execution, parallel contention, and memory-constrained execution, the agentic scheduler reaches the brute-force optimal mapping in all 13 scenarios, matching the best classical baseline on mapping accuracy while avoiding bandit-style exploration over complete mappings, and outperforming HEFT, StarPU, and the all-GPU policy while requiring zero offline training.

</details>


### [7] [NUMA balancing hampering performance of spiking network simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22275)
*Melissa Lober,Alp Inangu,Gorka Peraza Coppola,Dennis Terhorst,Sebastian Gillessen,Jan Vogelsang,Hans Ekkehard Plesser,Brian Wylie,Benedikt Steinbusch,Guido Trensch,Susanne Kunkel,Markus Diesmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 关闭自动NUMA平衡可降低大规模脉冲网络仿真30%的能耗，该效应此前被忽视但对HPC能耗优化具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态计算为人工智能提供了能效更高的替代架构，而在传统超算上模拟大规模脉冲网络是参考基准。研究旨在探究如何提高此类仿真的能效，发现自动NUMA平衡会导致时间测量波动，阻碍仿真技术优化，且该效应在现有文献中虽有迹象但未成为科学计算的常识。

Method: 开发了一种新的时间和计算节点分辨的性能显示方法，能够揭示分布式脉冲网络仿真的细粒度时间变异性；通过性能分析暴露自动NUMA平衡与线程感知内存分配库jemalloc之间的动态交互及其瞬态影响。

Result: 关闭自动NUMA平衡可使脉冲网络仿真的能耗降低30%，这一降幅在成本效益上远超其他能效提升尝试；性能波动源于内存访问模式与自动NUMA平衡的动态交互，不影响仿真结果正确性但会导致时间测量波动；已为超算配备按作业开关自动NUMA平衡的选项。

Conclusion: 自动NUMA平衡对脉冲网络仿真等科学计算具有负面影响，应在用户级别提供按需开关选项；该效应在科学计算领域知晓度不足，需要进一步调查其在科学代码中的普遍性。

Abstract: Computing centers today mostly operate conventional CPU- and GPU-based systems, where the direct way of decreasing energy consumption is a reduction in the applications' runtime. Neuromorphic computing promises an alternative architecture with improved energy efficiency for artificial intelligence. In this endeavor, code for the simulation of large-scale spiking networks on conventional supercomputers is the reference. We show that turning off automatic NUMA balancing may reduce energy consumption by 30%. This dwarfs other attempts of increasing the energy efficiency of a computing center with respect to cost effectiveness. The memory access pattern of spiking network simulation code dynamically interacts with automatic NUMA balancing. This does not affect the correctness of simulation results and thus goes unnoticed in day-to-day neuroscience research. In performance analysis, however, time measurements fluctuate obstructing attempts to optimize simulation technology. A new time- and compute-node resolved performance display exposes the fine-grained temporal variability of distributed spiking network simulations. The analysis uncovers that automatic NUMA balancing is of disadvantage and affects the jemalloc library for thread-aware memory allocation in a transient manner. The method also allows developers to detect perturbations of the HPC system and target specific improvements to simulation technology. As a consequence, we have equipped our supercomputers with an option to turn on or off automatic NUMA balancing on a per-job basis on the user level. This gives researchers the opportunity to find the best setting for the application at hand. There are indications in the literature that the effect has been observed before, yet it does not seem common knowledge in scientific computing. It remains to be investigated how widespread the phenomenon is among scientific codes.

</details>


### [8] [TileSight: A First-Principles Tile-Centric Analytical GPU Performance Model from Cores to Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22432)
*Zhiwen Mo,Yu Cheng,Lei Wang,Zhengju Tang,Lei Xu,Guoyu Li,Yuqi Dong,Lingxiao Ma,Yuqing Xia,Jilong Xue,Fan Yang,Luo Mai,Zhi Yang,Wayne Luk,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了TileSight，一个以tile为中心的GPU性能建模工具，在单GPU和多GPU场景下均实现了高精度的性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU性能分析工具落后于编程范式的发展。当前tile-centric编程框架（如Triton、TileLang）已成为主流，但性能分析工具仍依赖粗糙的roofline模型、不透明的ML预测器或事后分析器。这一差距在现代AI工作负载中尤为突出，因为kernel融合和分布式推理涉及复杂的tensor core、缓存层次和跨GPU通信。

Method: TileSight将tile从编程原语提升为分析原语，建立三层性能模型：(1) tile内层：将工作表示为跨网络、内存和计算流水线的资源向量；(2) tile间层：调度依赖和有序动作以暴露合法重叠，并从tile复用距离推断多级缓存命中率；(3)跨设备层：将远程张量访问映射到放置位置并通过alpha-beta阶段成本进行路由。核心创新在于在GPU核心内部建模计算-内存流水线重叠，跨核心建模缓存层次，跨GPU建模节点间通信。

Result: 在A100、H200、B200和B6000上的实验表明：单GPU kernel延迟预测的汇总平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)为12.35%，优于现有SOTA方法且具有更好的跨架构迁移能力；L2缓存命中率预测误差约1个百分点；在多达32个GPU上，融合分布式kernel的加权MAPE(wMAPE)为16.18%，端到端vLLM服务的wMAPE为13.52%；在优化任务中，TileSight选择的tile配置与厂商和专家基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: TileSight成功填补了tile-centric编程框架与性能分析工具之间的空白，通过统一的tile抽象实现了跨层次的性能建模，为现代AI工作负载提供了高效准确的性能预测和优化指导，证明了tile作为分析原语的有效性。

Abstract: Recent GPU programming frameworks such as Triton, TileLang, and CUDA Tile adopt tiles as first-class primitives, making tile-centric programming the prevailing approach for high-performance GPU kernels. Performance-analysis tooling has not followed: programmers still rely on coarse roofline bounds, opaque ML predictors, or post-hoc profilers to understand kernel execution. This gap is acute for modern AI workloads, where kernel fusion and distributed inference depend on tensor cores, CUDA cores, cache hierarchies, memory pipelines, and inter-GPU networks. We present TileSight, a tile-centric performance-modeling tool that elevates the tile from a programming primitive to an analysis primitive. Within a GPU core, TileSight models compute-memory pipeline overlap; across cores, it models the cache hierarchy; across GPUs, it models inter-node communication. All layers share the tile abstraction: the intra-tile layer expresses work as a resource vector spanning network, memory, and compute pipelines; the inter-tile layer schedules dependent and ordered actions to expose legal overlap and infers multi-level cache hit rates from tile reuse distance; and the cross-device layer maps remote tensor accesses to placements and routes them through an alpha-beta stage cost. On A100, H200, B200, and B6000, TileSight predicts single-GPU kernel latency with 12.35% pooled mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and transferring better across architectures. Its L2 cache-hit-rate predictions are within roughly one percentage point of measurements on every GPU. At up to 32 GPUs, TileSight achieves 16.18% weighted MAPE (wMAPE) on fused distributed kernels and 13.52% wMAPE on end-to-end vLLM serving. In optimization, TileSight selects tile configurations competitive with strong vendor and expert baselines. TileSight will be open-sourced upon publication.

</details>

### [9] [RED-PIM: Reducing Data Movement for Transformers using Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21731)
*Zahra Yousefijamarani,Alaa Alameldeen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RED-PIM，一种算法-架构协同设计方案，通过重组矩阵运算和优化数据传输策略，将注意力机制中的bank间数据移动从O(N²)降至O(N)，中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小至d×d，显著提升了基于PIM的Transformer推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在NLP、计算机视觉、网络搜索和DNA序列分析等多个领域应用广泛，但其注意力操作中处理单元和内存之间的大量数据移动严重限制了效率。虽然已有研究提出基于PIM的实现方案，但存在昂贵的bank间通信开销和因存储bank容量有限导致的扩展性问题，注意力相关数据被迫跨bank分割，削弱了PIM的潜在优势。

Method: 提出RED-PIM算法-架构协同设计方案：通过重组矩阵运算实现本地化计算，采用优化数据传输策略，最小化bank间数据移动（复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N)），并将中间注意力矩阵从N×N压缩至d×d，从而降低计算开销和互连流量。

Result: 与基准PIM实现相比，推理时间减少16.05%至99.99%（几何平均66.42%），长序列场景提升最为显著。在真实数据集上，长文档性能提升99.60%，短文档提升13.44%，同时保持或提升准确率。

Conclusion: RED-PIM有效解决了可扩展且高效的Transformer推理问题，证明了算法-架构协同设计在优化基于PIM的Transformer实现方面的有效性，尤其适用于长序列场景。

Abstract: Transformers are widely used across many domains, including natural language processing, computer vision, web search, and DNA sequence analysis. Given their broad applicability, improving the performance of transformer models is critical. However, the high volume of data movement between processing units and memory during attention operations significantly limits their efficiency. Processing-In-Memory (PIM) mitigates this issue by performing computations directly inside memory. While prior work has proposed PIM-based transformer implementations, they suffer from costly inter-bank communication, and struggle to scale due to the limited capacity of memory banks. As a result, attention-related data must be split across banks, diminishing the potential benefits of PIM.
  In this work, we propose RED-PIM, an algorithm-architecture co-design that reduces attention latency by minimizing inter-bank data movement from O(N^2) to O(N) and shrinking intermediate attention matrices from N x N to d x d. By reorganizing matrix operations, performing computations locally, and employing an optimized data transfer strategy, RED-PIM significantly reduces computation cost and interconnect traffic. Compared to baseline PIM implementation, RED-PIM achieves inference time reductions ranging from 16.05% to 99.99% (geometric mean of 66.42%), with the largest gains on longer sequences. On real-world datasets, RED-PIM improves performance by 99.60% for long documents and 13.44% for shorter ones, while maintaining or improving accuracy. These results demonstrate RED-PIM's effectiveness for scalable and efficient transformer inference.

</details>


### [10] [Cloud-Native Evaluation-as-a-Service: A Microservices Architecture for Scalable AI Monitoring with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21623)
*Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EaaS，一种云原生AI评估参考架构，通过六个Kubernetes微服务实现AI评估方法的运营化，并在覆盖性保证、缺失数据鲁棒性、漂移检测和公平性监控四个方面验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI评估工具缺乏将评估方法即服务化的能力，且未能有效结合共形预测、微服务分解和基于DAG的编排，这促使研究者开发一个云原生的AI评估平台，以实现评估方法的运营化和实时监控。

Method: 提出EaaS云原生参考架构，将AI评估方法实现为六个无状态Kubernetes微服务：具有有限样本校正自适应预测集的共形预测、校准评估、通过RFF近似最大均值差异的漂移检测、带bootstrap置信区间的公平性监控、基于DAG的管道编排器和结果存储API。通过K=50次随机校准/测试分割验证了覆盖性、缺失数据鲁棒性、漂移检测能力和公平性监控效果。

Result: 实验验证表明：(1)在50次分割中，经验覆盖率与边际共形保证一致，平均覆盖率在名义目标的1.4个百分点内；(2)所有四个MMLU答案标记出现在top-20 logprobs中，无需填补，模拟10%填补产生小于1.5%的覆盖率影响；(3)RFF-MMD在中位数启发式带宽下对轻度和严重漂移实现100%检测能力，I类误差在5-8.5%之间；(4)公平性监控揭示了显著的种族人口均等差异(DP差距=0.33)；(5)共形预测和校准服务在批量大小100时实现亚2ms的p99延迟，RFF-MMD需要约500ms适合定期批量监控。

Conclusion: EaaS成功实现了AI评估方法的运营化，提供了云原生、微服务架构的评估平台，在覆盖性、鲁棒性、漂移检测和公平性监控方面表现优异。与四个开源工具的比较表明，目前没有平台能够结合共形预测即服务、微服务分解和基于DAG的编排，填补了这一技术空白。

Abstract: We present EaaS, a cloud-native reference architecture that operationalizes AI evaluation methods as six stateless Kubernetes microservices: conformal prediction with finite-sample-corrected Adaptive Prediction Sets, calibration assessment, drift detection via RFF-approximated Maximum Mean Discrepancy, fairness monitoring with bootstrap confidence intervals, a DAG-based pipeline orchestrator, and a result storage API. We validate four key methodological concerns. First, empirical coverage is consistent with the marginal conformal guarantee across K=50 random calibration/test splits, with mean coverage within 1.4 percentage points of the nominal target. Second, all four MMLU answer tokens appear in the top-20 logprobs with 0% imputation needed, and simulated imputation at 10% produces less than 1.5% coverage impact. Third, RFF-MMD achieves 100% detection power for mild and severe drift at the median heuristic bandwidth, with Type I error between 5-8.5%. Fourth, fairness monitoring on the UCI Adult Income dataset reveals significant demographic parity disparities by race (DP gap=0.33) with stable alerts across sequential batches. Conformal prediction and calibration services achieve sub-2ms p99 latency at batch size 100; RFF-MMD requires ~500ms suited for periodic batch monitoring. A comparison with four open-source tools suggests that, to the best of our knowledge, no current platform combines conformal-prediction-as-a-service, microservice decomposition, and DAG-based orchestration.

</details>


### [11] [On the Depth Scalability of Logic Gate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21633)
*Taegun An,Dohun kim,Haebeom Lee,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGN），通过在每一层引入直接输入锚定，解决了传统LGNs无法从深度增加中获益的问题，实现了超过100层的深度扩展和准确性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逻辑门网络（LGNs）无法可靠地从增加深度中获益。本文识别了两个原因：深层松弛LGNs中的优化崩溃，以及即使通过跳跃偏置初始化和直通估计稳定训练后仍然存在的拓扑限制。仅具备可训练性是不够的，深层还需要接收能够支持有用计算的信息。

Method: 提出输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGNs），其中每个门将演化的隐藏特征与直接的输入锚定相结合。该拓扑结构保持计算主干，同时使每一层都基于原始输入进行条件化。证明了深度为D的路径可以依赖于多达D+1个输入位，建立了严格的路径深度层次。引入随机k锚定松弛来改进锚定选择。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，IALGNs在固定宽度下实现了超过100层的持续深度-准确性提升，而替代LGN拓扑则出现饱和或退化。层次探测、拓扑消融和有效深度分析表明，输入锚定产生了逐步更具信息量的表示并保持了更长的计算路径。

Conclusion: LGNs中的可扩展深度既需要稳定的优化，也需要支持输入条件细化的信息访问模式。IALGNs成功实现了这一点，证明了深度增加可以带来实际的性能提升。

Abstract: Logic Gate Networks (LGNs) implement computation through compositions of Boolean operations, yet unlike classical Boolean circuits, existing LGNs do not reliably benefit from increased depth. We identify two distinct causes: optimization collapse in deep relaxed LGNs and a topology-induced limitation that persists even when skip-biased initialization and straight-through estimation stabilize training. Thus, trainability alone is insufficient; deeper layers must also receive information that supports useful computation.
  We introduce Input-Anchored Logic Gate Networks (IALGNs), in which each gate combines an evolving hidden feature with a direct input anchor. This topology preserves a computational spine while conditioning every layer on the original input. We show that a depth-D path can depend on up to D+1 input bits and establish a strict path-wise depth hierarchy. Random-k anchor relaxation further improves anchor selection without relaxing the spine.
  Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, IALGNs achieve consistent fixed-width depth--accuracy improvements beyond 100 layers, whereas alternative LGN topologies saturate or degrade. Layer-wise probes, topology ablations, and effective-depth analysis show that input anchoring produces progressively more informative representations and preserves longer computational paths. These results demonstrate that scalable depth in LGNs requires both stable optimization and an information-access pattern that supports input-conditioned refinement.

</details>


### [12] [MotifRole-Diff: Risk-Optimal Role-Aware Corruption for Masked Molecular Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21634)
*Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousef*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MotifRole-Diff，一种角色感知的分子图生成掩码策略，通过为不同类型token分配不同掩码率来提升生成质量，在QM9和MOSES数据集上显著提高了有效性和化学相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子图生成的掩码离散扩散方法对所有token使用统一的腐蚀策略，忽略了分子结构中不同组件token在去噪难度和对分子结构影响方面的显著差异，这种同等处理方式不够高效。

Method: 提出角色感知的腐蚀过程MotifRole-Diff，根据实证测量的去噪难度和图级扰动影响为不同token角色分配掩码率，将调度选择建模为固定掩码预算在token角色间的风险最优分配问题，并给出角色加权残差风险的优化定理。

Result: 在相同架构和计算预算下，QM9数据集有效性从0.905提升至0.944，FCD从1.701降至1.609；MOSES数据集有效性从0.920提升至0.938，FCD从2.125降至1.850；各分子图token类别的重建性能均有改善。

Conclusion: 结构引导的腐蚀策略比统一掩码调度更适合序列化分子图扩散任务，证明了角色感知掩码策略的有效性。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion for molecular graph generation typically applies a uniform corruption schedule to all tokens in a lossless graph-to-sequence representation, implicitly treating structurally heterogeneous molecular components as equally difficult and equally important to reconstruct. However, different molecular graph token roles exhibit substantial variation in denoising difficulty and their influence on the decoded molecule, motivating role-specific corruption strategies. We introduce MotifRole-Diff, a role-aware corruption process that allocates masking rates according to empirically measured denoising difficulty and graph-level perturbation impact while preserving the model architecture, clean sequence space, and lossless molecular-graph decoder. We formulate schedule selection as the risk-optimal allocation of a fixed masking budget across token roles. Our theorem characterizes optimality for the modeled role-weighted residual risk, while downstream generation performance is evaluated empirically. Under matched architecture, training budget, and sampling compute, MotifRole-Diff improves validity on QM9 from 0.905 to 0.944 while reducing FCD from 1.701 to 1.609, and on MOSES improves validity from 0.920 to 0.938 while reducing FCD from 2.125 to 1.850. Role-wise diagnostics further show improved reconstruction across molecular graph token categories. Together, these matched-compute results indicate that structurally informed corruption is a more effective masking strategy than uniform schedules for serialized molecular graph diffusion.

</details>


### [13] [Toward User-Conditioned Evaluation of Personal LLM Agents under Temporal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21635)
*Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Qiaolin Yu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Zhitong Guo,Zhicheng Wang,Junxian You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前个人智能体基准测试割裂了各能力评估，提出需满足时序干预、持久状态、跨维度效应和用户条件状态变化四个条件，但审查发现现有基准均不符合，并给出了未来的基准设计要求和指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试（工具、记忆、安全）通常孤立评估各项能力，无法捕捉个人智能体随时间积累的持久状态与用户定制化带来的复杂交互与故障传播，缺乏对跨组件影响的评估。

Method: 形式化提出个人智能体评估协议需满足的四个条件：显式时序干预、跨干预的持久状态、引发的跨维度效应、以及用户条件化状态的变化；对公开基准协议进行有纳入标准的集中审计。

Result: 在所审计的协议集中，未发现同时满足四个条件的基准，仅存在若干近似案例；该结论为聚焦性的缺口分析，文献覆盖有界。

Conclusion: 提出了一套最小基准设计方案与候选报告指标，为未来个人智能体的用户条件化适应评估提供了明确的设计要求与度量工具。

Abstract: Personal agents maintain memories, learned skills, tool configurations, and policy state that evolve with each user. Existing agent benchmarks often evaluate these capabilities in isolation: tool benchmarks test invocation under fixed APIs, memory benchmarks test recall or forgetting, and safety benchmarks test static policy compliance. We argue that personal-agent evaluation requires a different protocol: replaying the same temporal intervention across different persistent user-conditioned states and measuring how failures propagate across agent components. We formalize this requirement as four conditions: explicit temporal intervention, persistent state across the intervention, induced cross-dimensional effects, and variation in user-conditioned state. A focused audit of public benchmark protocols selected by explicit inclusion criteria identifies several close cases. Under our explicitly narrow operationalization, we did not find a protocol in that audited set satisfying all four conditions. This claim is scoped as a focused gap analysis with bounded literature coverage. This position paper proposes a minimal benchmark design and candidate reporting metrics for user-conditioned adaptation. The result is a concrete design requirement for future personal-agent evaluation, with metrics used as reporting tools for that requirement.

</details>


### [14] [Measuring the Dependency Gap: Diagnosing Inter-Column Fidelity in Tabular Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21636)
*Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现现有合成表格数据评估指标对列间依赖关系不敏感，提出新的依赖感知诊断方法，发现最先进生成器存在依赖缺口，且常规改进方法均无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成表格数据在欺诈检测和临床风险评估等不平衡领域中，列间依赖关系对少数类别的判别信号至关重要。然而，作者发现现有最常用的评估指标（如逻辑回归C2ST和成对Trend分数）对列间依赖关系不敏感，无法有效评估合成数据是否保留了这种关键结构。

Method: 作者提出了一个依赖感知的保真度诊断方法，将强分类器二样本检验（XGB-C2ST）分解为边际分布、依赖关系和数值-类别交叉三个组件。该方法以完全分解的参考基准（所有依赖被破坏）作为最坏情况，以真实数据作为最优情况进行锚定定位。作者将其应用于最先进的流匹配生成器TabbyFlow/EF-VFM进行验证。

Result: 标准评估指标遗漏了生成器存在的真实依赖缺口；破坏依赖关系会导致少数类别效用完全崩溃。更重要的是，该依赖缺口具有顽固性：模型容量增加16倍无法弥补，模型内依赖机制、事后copula校正等方法均无效。研究证明该缺口并非源于平均场生成目标的结构性限制（目标具有渐近精确性），而是源于缺乏直接依赖监督。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了合成表格数据评估和生成中的关键盲点，挑战了该领域关于常规改进方法有效的假设。发现依赖缺口源于缺乏直接监督而非容量或结构限制，且由于该缺口是高阶的，廉价干预无法解决，这对该领域提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is valued for preserving not only each column's marginal distribution but the dependencies between columns -- structure that carries much of the discriminative signal for minority classes in imbalanced domains such as fraud and clinical risk. Yet the metrics most commonly used to certify synthetic tabular data are, we show, largely blind to inter-column dependency: a baseline that models every column independently (and therefore destroys all dependency) is judged indistinguishable from real data by the logistic-regression C2ST, and the pairwise Trend score is only partially sensitive. We introduce a dependency-aware fidelity diagnostic that decomposes a strong classifier two-sample test (XGB-C2ST) into marginal, dependency, and numerical-categorical cross components, anchored between a worst-case fully-factorized reference (all dependency destroyed) and a best-case real-data oracle. Applying it to a state-of-the-art flow-matching generator (TabbyFlow/EF-VFM), we find a real dependency gap that standard metrics miss; destroying dependency outright collapses minority-class utility, and the generator's residual gap carries a smaller, consistent utility cost. We then ask whether this gap reflects a structural limitation of mean-field generative objectives. It does not: consistent with recent recovery results for variational flow matching, the objective is asymptotically exact. Yet the gap is stubborn -- a 16x increase in model capacity does not close it -- pointing to the absence of direct dependency supervision rather than a capacity or structural limit. Consistent with this, and because the residual gap is higher-order, no cheap intervention closes it: not an in-model dependency mechanism, not post-hoc copula correction, and not the 16x capacity increase -- a caution for a field that assumes such fixes help.

</details>


### [15] [Quasi-Monte Carlo Initialization for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21637)
*Julian G. Soltes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化在元强化学习中的效果，发现其在相似任务上优于现代正交方法，但在不相似任务上正交方法更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化方法在现代基准环境中的元强化学习效果，以改进训练收敛性能并寻找更优的先验初始化策略。

Method: 采用多种采样方法来约束基于群体的搜索，从基准任务集合中聚合最优先验；将QMC元先验与现代正交（SB3）默认初始化方法进行对比实验。

Result: 在迁移到相似的未见连续控制环境时，QMC元先验相比现代正交（SB3）默认方法展现出更好的训练收敛性能；但在不相似任务中，正交初始化在全局上更具优势，适合无偏搜索。

Conclusion: QMC权重初始化在元强化学习中具有任务依赖的有效性：对于相似任务迁移场景能加速收敛，而对于差异性较大的任务，正交初始化方法仍然更为稳健。

Abstract: This paper explores the efficacy of quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC) weight initialization for meta-reinforcement learning within modern benchmark environments. Various sampling methods are used to bound a population-based search and aggregate an optimal prior from a baseline set of tasks. The QMC meta-priors show improvements in training convergence compared to modern orthogonal (SB3) defaults when extrapolated to similar unseen continuous control environments. In dissimilar tasks, the orthogonal orientation was globally superior for an unbiased search.

</details>


### [16] [Toward Goal-Agnostic Joint-Embedding Predictive Control of Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21644)
*Jonathan Gallagher,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于JEPA的目标无关PDE控制框架，通过冻结的潜在动力学模型和物理观测变量（如动能探针）实现高效控制，在Navier-Stokes基准上优于直接在潜在空间规划的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PDE控制方法通常需要针对特定目标进行训练，缺乏泛化能力。本研究旨在开发一种目标无关的控制框架，能够在不指定奖励或下游目标的情况下学习通用表征，并支持多种控制任务的灵活应用。

Method: 构建基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)的控制框架：使用小型2D ViT编码器和动作条件的潜在动力学模型进行离线训练，训练完成后冻结模型参数。采用模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器，通过学习得到的线性动能探针在冻结的潜在展开上进行规划，而非直接最小化潜在空间的欧氏距离。

Result: 在PDE Control Gym的2D Navier-Stokes基准测试中：动能探针规划将50回合奖励从-12.08±0.86提升至-10.90±0.91；末段速度场RMSE从0.0765降至0.0692。在三个异质非周期目标上，动能规划将末段场RMSE降低53%（0.0220 vs 0.0469），30次配对试验全胜。稳态控制的平均相对误差仅2.7%。轨迹复现R²达0.989。

Conclusion: 验证了潜在动力学可以同时保持动态特性和目标无关性，但经过校准的物理观测变量（在保证唯一延拓的前提下）更适合作为状态控制的目标函数。该方法为PDE控制提供了一种可复用、目标无关的有效框架。

Abstract: We present a goal-agnostic control framework for partial differential equations (PDEs) built around a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA). The small 2D ViT encoder and action-conditioned latent dynamics are trained offline without a reward or downstream goal, frozen, and reused by a model-predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. We find that when available, the control objective is better applied to an explicit physical observable (provided injectivity) than to minimizing raw Euclidean distance ($L^2$) in the learned latent space. For a learned linear kinetic-energy (KE) probe on frozen latent rollouts we can reproduce held-out trajectories with $R^2=0.989$, while requiring no change to the underlying world model. On the PDE Control Gym 2D Navier--Stokes benchmark, using KE-probe planning improves the matched 50-episode native reward from $-12.08\pm0.86$ for latent-$L^2$ planning to $-10.90\pm0.91$ (95\% CI), while lowering last-quarter velocity-field RMSE from $0.0765$ to $0.0692$. Across three intentionally withheld, dissimilar, aperiodic targets, KE planning lowers late field RMSE by $53\%$ relative to latent-$L^2$ planning ($0.0220$ versus $0.0469$), winning all 30 paired episodes. The same frozen model also supports controls targeting stabilization around a steady configuration via direct regulation of KE achieving $2.7\%$ mean relative error. While the latent probe is brittle to measurement noise and missing pixels, we believe the results support the claim that latent dynamics can remain both dynamic and goal-agnostic while calibrated observables (granted they guarantee unique continuation) may be a better objective for state control

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多视野潜在一致性(lambda参数)对视频预测器过渡几何的影响，发现在Moving-MNIST上提高lambda可显著降低扩张代理指标和预测误差，但这种效果存在领域限制，并通过随机强制定律统一了不同控制领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视野潜在一致性是视频预测器和世界模型中常见的训练手段，但实践者很少了解它对过渡几何的影响机制。作者将多步潜在一致性权重lambda作为诊断控制变量，旨在揭示其如何影响模型的过渡动力学特性。

Method: 1. 将lambda(多步潜在一致性权重)作为诊断控制变量进行系统性实验
2. 测量经验扩张代理指标L20,q95和地平线20预测误差E20作为评估指标
3. 在多个数据集上进行实验：Moving-MNIST、Pendulum-v1、CartPole-v1、KTH Actions
4. 进行关联中介分析、防御性检查(架构基线、外部压力、WorldTest、MPC、缩放)
5. 建立随机强制定律L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82η来统一不同控制领域

Result: 1. 在Moving-MNIST上，lambda从0增至0.8时，L20从4.96±2.01降至1.01±0.06(配对t检验p=0.005)，E20减半(0.365→0.177)
2. 六个种子中有四个在lambda=0.8时达到L<1
3. 相同损失在Pendulum-v1、CartPole-v1和KTH Actions上未能产生L<1，即使E20有改善
4. 中介分析显示r-hat=0.94(95% CI [0.88, 1.00])
5. 发现随机强制定律L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82η(bootstrap斜率CI [1.73, 1.92]，R²=0.96)可统一控制领域
6. 在lambda=0.4和1.2时观察到类似的线性L20(η)斜率(~1.69和~2.00)

Conclusion: 软一致性可以将被动视频推向近收缩带，但这种效果存在领域限制。研究支持一个有限声明：软一致性能够推动被动视频趋向近收缩区域，但该区域受到领域限制。通过校准的η_eff，随机强制定律成功统一了不同控制领域在同一曲线上。研究未报告DreamerV3或TD-MPC2的回报结果。

Abstract: Multi-horizon latent consistency is a common training knob in video predictors and world models, but practitioners rarely know what it does to transition geometry. We treat lambda, the weight on multi-step latent agreement, as a diagnostic control and measure an empirical expansion proxy L20,q95 together with horizon-20 prediction error E20. On Moving-MNIST (n=6 seeds at the critical pair), raising lambda from 0 to 0.8 cuts L20 from 4.96 +/- 2.01 to 1.01 +/- 0.06 (paired t p=0.005, Wilcoxon p=0.031) and halves E20 (0.365 to 0.177, paired t p=1.1e-13). Four of six seeds cross L<1 at lambda=0.8. The same loss does not produce population L<1 on action-conditioned Pendulum-v1 or CartPole-v1, nor on KTH Actions video, even when E20 improves. An associational mediation analysis on MMNIST gives r-hat=0.94 (95% CI [0.88, 1.00], n=27, B=2000); lambda was not randomized. Defensive checks (architectural baselines, exogenous stress, WorldTest, MPC, scaling) mostly support a narrow claim: soft consistency can push passive video toward a near-contractive band, and that band is domain-limited. A stochastic-forcing law L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82 eta at lambda=0.8 (bootstrap slope CI [1.73, 1.92], R^2=0.96) unifies control domains on the same curve via calibrated eta_eff. Complete joint slices at lambda in {0.4, 1.2} (30/30 cells, 5 eta x 3 seeds) show comparable linear L20(eta) slopes (~1.69 and ~2.00); we do not fit a continuous (lambda, eta) surface. We do not report DreamerV3 or TD-MPC2 returns.

</details>


### [18] [Adjustment Speed as a Safety Constraint for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21646)
*Timothy Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将调整速度作为非平稳强化学习的安全约束，通过预测适应需求与恢复能力的对比，在环境变化期间主动收紧动作集并激活屏蔽机制，从而减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全强化学习方法通常假设平稳环境，未将适应速度视为安全问题。然而当环境随时间演化时，延迟适应可能导致瞬态不安全行为，因此需要在非平稳环境中确保学习系统能在恢复期限内安全适应预测的环境变化。

Method: 提出适应可行性作为安全定义：当保持安全所需的适应超过学习系统的恢复能力时，未来状态变得不安全。框架利用学习的上下文表示和短期上下文预测估计适应需求，与智能体的适应能力进行比较。当预测的适应需求超过恢复能力时，主动收紧可允许动作集并激活动作级屏蔽机制。

Result: 在非平稳驾驶环境实验中，该方法主要在与上下文变化对齐的短期窗口内减少安全违规。消融研究表明屏蔽机制在峰值和尾部风险抑制上更保守，优化级调整提供了短期切换条件违规的额外减少。

Conclusion: 适应可行性可作为非平稳强化学习的实用安全原则，主动干预能在环境变化期间提高安全性，证明了将调整速度作为安全约束的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in reinforcement learning under nonstationarity requires determining whether a learning system can safely adapt to forecasted environmental change within the required recovery horizon. Existing safe reinforcement learning methods typically assume stationary environments and do not explicitly consider adaptation speed as a safety concern. However, when environments evolve over time, delayed adaptation may result in transient unsafe behavior.
  This paper proposes adjustment speed as a safety constraint for nonstationary reinforcement learning. The central idea is to define safety in terms of adaptation feasibility: future states or regions may become unsafe when the adaptation required to remain safe exceeds the learning system's calibrated recovery capacity. The proposed framework uses learned context representations and short-horizon context forecasts to estimate adaptation demand and compare it with the agent's achievable adaptation capacity.
  When predicted adaptation demand exceeds the calibrated recovery capacity, the framework proactively tightens the admissible action set and activates an action-level shield to reduce unsafe behavior before violations occur.
  Experiments in a nonstationary driving environment show that the proposed approach primarily reduces safety violations in short-horizon windows aligned with context changes. Ablation studies further show that shielding is more conservative for peak- and tail-risk suppression, while optimization-level adjustment provides additional reductions in short-horizon switch-conditioned violations.
  These results support adaptation feasibility as a practical safety principle for reinforcement learning under nonstationarity and demonstrate that proactive intervention can improve safety during periods of environmental change.

</details>


### [19] [Molt: A Scalable PyTorch-Native Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21653)
*Jian Hu,Huiying Li,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yifan Zhang,Shaokun Zhang,Hemil Desai,Michael Demoret,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Molt是一个轻量级的PyTorch原生Agentic强化学习训练框架，旨在降低研究迭代成本，在保持性能的同时实现代码简洁和端到端可修改性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流强化学习框架中，每次算法修改都需要穿透训练器、分布式后端和rollout粘合层，研究迭代成本高昂。现有框架复杂度高，研究者难以全面理解，AI编码助手也难以有效推理。

Method: 设计PyTorch原生框架，采用单一异步循环训练多模态和混合专家策略，确保代理作为普通程序运行。框架保持代码库紧凑清晰，支持端到端流程追踪和修改，保证token一致性、策略版本一致性和模型语义一致性。

Result: 在匹配的完全异步协议下，Molt的性能与基于Megatron的先进技术栈统计相当。框架成功实现代码简洁性与高性能的平衡，支持多模态和MoE策略训练。

Conclusion: Molt证明了轻量级设计不必牺牲性能，为研究者提供了易于理解和修改的强化学习训练环境，已开源并提供完整配方和容器支持。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning research is constant algorithm modification, new estimators, new pipeline stages, new rollout schemes, and in mainstream frameworks each change threads through layers of trainer, distributed backend, and rollout glue: the cost lands on the researcher at every iteration. Molt is a PyTorch-native training framework built to keep that cost small: a codebase compact and clean enough for a researcher to hold in their head, and for an AI coding assistant to read and reason about in its entirety, so the algorithm flow can be traced and changed end to end. The agent is an ordinary program, and one asynchronous loop trains multimodal and mixture-of-experts policies while never training on a token it did not generate, consistent in tokens, policy versions, and model semantics. Leanness does not cost performance: under a matched, fully asynchronous protocol, Molt is statistically comparable to a state-of-the-art Megatron-based stack. Molt is open source and provides recipes and containers at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt.

</details>


### [20] [A Drift Stable Quantum Federated Learning for Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21647)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DUQFL-Prox框架，通过深度展开的局部优化和近端项来解决量子联邦学习中的数据异构性和噪声优化问题，在金融欺诈检测和基因组分类任务上实现了更好的稳定性、泛化性能和客户端公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习在隐私敏感的分布式智能服务中具有重要应用价值，但面临着客户端数据异构性和量子优化噪声导致的本地更新不稳定、客户端漂移以及客户端间性能不公平等关键挑战，这些问题严重影响了系统的可靠性和公平性。

Method: 提出了基于深度展开局部优化的漂移稳定量子联邦学习框架DUQFL-Prox。核心方法包括：（1）每个客户端采用自适应展开的SPSA（同步扰动随机近似）更新替代固定优化器；（2）引入近端项约束本地模型靠近全局模型以减轻客户端漂移；（3）设计轻量级控制器学习步骤特定的优化参数以提升聚合后性能。

Result: 在金融欺诈检测和基因组分类两个实际任务上的实验表明，DUQFL-Prox相比标准QFL基线方法在稳定性、泛化能力和客户端公平性方面均有显著提升，证明了深度展开量子联邦学习方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度展开的量子联邦学习方法能够有效支持异构分布式环境中更可靠、更公平的智能服务，为隐私敏感的分布式决策系统（如欺诈检测和基因组分类）提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables distributed clients to train quantum neural networks without sharing local data, making it promising for privacy-aware intelligent services. Intelligent services in this context refer to privacy-sensitive distributed decision systems, such as fraud detection and genomic classification, where reliable and fair client-level learning is as important as the accuracy of the aggregate model. However, heterogeneous client data and noisy quantum optimization often cause unstable local updates, client drift, and unfair performance between clients. This paper proposes DUQFL-Prox, a drift-stable quantum federated learning framework based on deep-unfolded local optimization. Instead of using a fixed local optimizer, each client performs adaptive unfolded SPSA updates, while a proximal term keeps the local model close to the global model. A lightweight controller learns step-specific optimization parameters to improve post-aggregation performance. Experiments on financial fraud and genomic classification tasks show that DUQFL-Prox improves stability, generalization, and client fairness compared with standard QFL baselines. The results suggest that deep-unfolded quantum federated learning can support more reliable and fair intelligent services in heterogeneous distributed environments.

</details>


### [21] [Shallower ReLU Network Representations via Exact Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21651)
*Kilian Rueß,Gennadiy Averkov,Florestan Brunck,Moritz Grillo,Christoph Hertrich,Georg Loho,Jack Stade,Moritz Stargalla,Matthew Sun,Martin Winter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对n≤10的最大值函数可用两层隐藏层的ReLU网络精确表示，并给出了任意n的深度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ReLU网络表示最大值函数所需的最小深度是神经网络表示理论中的基础问题。先前工作仅证明了max_5可用两层隐藏层表示，对于更大的n缺乏精确结果。本文旨在突破这一限制，建立更广泛的深度上界。

Method: 通过将问题转化为精确有理线性代数问题，在对称性约简后，将必要的消去编码为Q上的有限线性系统，并通过计算方法求解和验证。max_10的构造采用仅含两两最大值的结构化第一隐藏层，可递归代入更大网络。利用广义铰链超平面表示理论，将结果扩展到连续分段线性函数。

Result: 证明了对所有n≤10，max_n可用两层隐藏层ReLU网络精确表示；对任意n>10，max_n可用⌈log_5(n/2)⌉+1层隐藏层表示。对R^d上的连续分段线性函数，深度上界为⌈log_5((d+1)/2)⌉+1，特别地，d≤9时可用两层隐藏层表示。

Conclusion: 显著改进了先前工作的结果，将两层隐藏层可表示的最大值函数从max_5扩展到max_10，并将一般情况的深度上界从⌈log_3(n-2)⌉+1改进到⌈log_5(n/2)⌉+1。

Abstract: We prove that the maximum of $n$ real numbers is exactly representable by a ReLU network with two hidden layers for every $n\le 10$. The constructions are obtained by reducing the problem to exact rational linear algebra: after a symmetry reduction, the necessary cancellations are encoded in finite linear systems over $\mathbb{Q}$, which we solve and verify computationally. The representation of $\max_{10}$ has a structured first hidden layer consisting only of pairwise maxima, a feature that allows it to be recursively substituted into larger networks. We use this to show that for every $n>10$, the maximum $\max_{n}$ can be exactly represented with $\lceil{\log_5 (n / 2)\rceil}+1 < \log_5(n) +1.5694$ hidden layers. Via the generalized hinging-hyperplane representation [Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005], the same depth bound holds for all continuous piecewise-linear functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$, with $d+1$ in place of $n$. In particular, every continuous piecewise-linear function on $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d\le 9$ admits a two-hidden-layer ReLU representation. Our results improve on [Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff, STOC'26]. In that work, the authors established a two-hidden-layer representation for $\max_{5}$ and an upper bound of $\lceil{\log_3 (n-2)\rceil}+1$ hidden layers for $\max_{n}$.

</details>


### [22] [Physically Constrained Federated Additive Models for O-RAN SLA-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21665)
*Aubida A. Al-Hameed,Mohammed M. H. Qazzaz,Maryam Hafeez,Syed A. Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Monotone FedNAM，一种物理约束的联邦加性模型，用于O-RAN服务保障中可审计的SLA违规预测，解决了传统NAM模型违背无线物理规律的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O-RAN主动服务保障中，需要预测每切片SLA违规。预测模型需满足：(1)可被运营商审计；(2)跨基站训练但不能汇集各切片KPI（商业敏感信息）。现有NAM模型虽具可见性，但在ColO-RAN数据集上学习到违背无线物理的效果（如信道质量改善却预测更高风险），在本地和集中训练下均出现此问题，非IID联邦平均更严重。

Method: 提出Monotone FedNAM联邦加性模型：将物理方向明确的KPI表示为单调样条函数，其约束在FedAvg聚合中构造性保持；将存在争议的KPI保持无约束。模型作为Non-RT RIC rApp训练运行，紧凑到可部署为Near-RT RIC xApp。

Result: 在ColO-RAN测试床数据集上：(1)消除所有单调性违规；(2)约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00；(3)泛化到未见过的调度策略；(4)上行流量减少65%；(5)AUC代价仅0.04-0.07。

Conclusion: 物理约束的联邦加性模型能够支持多租户O-RAN服务保障的可审计SLA风险推断，在保持预测性能的同时确保模型符合无线物理规律，实现了可审计性与物理有效性的统一。

Abstract: Proactive service assurance in O-RAN requires predicting per-slice SLA violations before they occur. The prediction model must be auditable by operators and must train across base stations without pooling per-slice KPIs, which are commercially sensitive because slices are leased to individual tenants. Neural additive models (NAMs) offer auditability because each KPI contributes through a visible shape function. However, visibility alone does not guarantee physical validity. On the ColO-RAN testbed dataset, unconstrained NAMs learn effects that contradict wireless physics, for example predicting higher risk when channel quality improves. This failure appears under both local and centralized training, and non-IID federated averaging worsens it. We present Monotone FedNAM, a federated additive model in which KPIs with unambiguous physical direction are represented as monotone splines whose constraints survive FedAvg aggregation by construction, while contestable KPIs remain unconstrained. The model trains and operates as a Non-RT RIC rApp and is compact enough for deployment as a Near-RT RIC xApp. Monotone FedNAM eliminates all monotonicity violations, raises constrained shape consistency from 0.71 to 1.00, generalizes to an unseen scheduling policy, and reduces uplink traffic by 65%, at a cost of 0.04 to 0.07 AUC. These results show that physically constrained federated additive models can support auditable SLA risk inference for multi-tenant O-RAN service assurance

</details>


### [23] [Neural Feature Governance: Extending Atom Prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21671)
*Idris Karel Seunda Ekwe,Patrick Tenga Shako,Ernest Parfait Fokoué*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了神经原子流行度（NAP）框架，一种用于前馈神经网络结构化节点级模型选择的贝叶斯方法，在保持高精度的同时实现了极高结构稀疏度（MNIST上活跃节点仅8%）和良好的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中，十亿参数级架构在获得高精度的同时，面临着缺乏透明度、计算效率低下和不确定性量化不可靠等问题，神经网络压缩和可解释性仍然是开放挑战。

Method: 提出神经原子流行度（NAP）框架，通过四阶段管道实现：1）通过迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）进行贝叶斯彩票（BLT）识别；2）Spike and Slab独立高斯（SS-IG）模型的软变分训练；3）泊松-二项（PB）最优层尺寸选择；4）贝叶斯微调以产生稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。

Result: 在模拟非线性回归、两个UCI基准数据集（Concrete、YearPredictionMSD）和MNIST图像分类任务上进行了广泛验证。NAP实现了最先进的结构稀疏度，在MNIST上将活跃节点减少到原始密集架构的8%；不确定性分解显示模型无知仅占总预测方差的3-4%；回归可靠性图显示预测区间覆盖率为93.4%（目标95%）。

Conclusion: NAP被确立为贝叶斯神经网络中同时追求稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化的可靠、理论基础扎实且计算可行的方法，为解决神经网络压缩和可解释性挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural network compression and interpretability remain open challenges in modern deep learn- ing, where billion-parameter architectures deliver impressive accuracy at the cost of trans- parency, computational efficiency, and reliable uncertainty quantification. This paper introduces Neural Atom Prevalence (NAP), a principled Bayesian framework for structured node-level model selection in feedforward neural networks. NAP introduces the neural atom (activation unit) and functions as a hybrid method operating through a four-phase pipeline: Bayesian Lottery Ticket (BLT) identification via Iterative Magnitude Pruning (IMP), soft variational training of the Spike and Slab Independent Gaussian (SS-IG) model, Poisson-Binomial (PB) optimal layer-size selection, and Bayesian fine-tuning to produce a sparse, stable, interpretable, and accurate model. Extensive empirical validation across simulated nonlinear regression, two UCI benchmark datasets (Concrete, YearPredictionMSD), and the MNIST image classification task demonstrates that NAP achieves state-of-the-art structural sparsity, reducing active nodes to as few as 8% of the original dense architecture on MNIST, while well-calibrated probabilisti- cally: the aleatoric-epistemic uncertainty decomposition reveals that model ignorance accounts for only 3 to 4% of total predictive variance across all experiments, and regression reliability diagrams confirm a near-nominal predictive interval coverage (93.4% observed against a 95% target). These results establish NAP as a reliable, theoretically grounded, and computation- ally tractable solution to the simultaneous pursuit of sparsity, accuracy, interpretability, and uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应OOD检测器通过无标签流数据更新记忆库，但其动力学行为缺乏理论理解。作者旨在揭示记忆库污染的演化规律，理解检测器崩溃的临界条件，并探索在无标签条件下自适应OOD检测的理论边界。

Result: 实验结果表明：1. 准入核斜率在所有编码器族中均接近1（协议签名），验证检测器类近临界设计；2. 在96个设置中预测阈值与经验崩溃点吻合，未门控字典损失高达0.163 AUROC；3. 认证准入门控在所有污染率下消除相变并控制假阳性；4. CDC在所有测试漂移影响单元上恢复名义FPR；5. 所提方法逼近理论功率上限。

Conclusion: 本文首次完整刻画了无标签自适应OOD检测的可能性与不可能性边界：证明了记忆库污染的临界动力学规律，提出了有效的门控解决方案，同时揭示了漂移与污染在无标签条件下不可区分这一根本限制，为该领域提供了理论指导和实践方法。

Abstract: Test-time adaptive out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors update a memory bank from the unlabelled stream. We show this adaptation obeys a provable dynamical law. Modelling bank impurity as a generalized Pólya urn, we prove almost-sure convergence to a mean-field equilibrium whose slope acts as a reproduction number. Below one, impurity stays benign. Above one, the bank is fully poisoned and the detector collapses. The measured admission kernel is affine ($R^2 \ge 0.996$) with slope just below one in every encoder family (a protocol signature), so this detector class is near-critical by design, and across 96 settings the predicted threshold matches the empirical collapse, where ungated dictionaries lose up to $0.163$ AUROC. We then prove that a certified admission gate, reading only a frozen reserve, severs the feedback loop and removes the transition at every contamination rate, even adversarially, while controlling false positives label-free. For the complementary static-calibration failure under drift we give CDC, which restores nominal FPR label-free on all tested drift-affected cells. Finally we prove a two-world impossibility theorem. Drift and contamination are indistinguishable without labels, forcing a closed-form power ceiling our procedure approaches. Together these give a complete possibility/impossibility characterization of label-free adaptive OOD detection.

</details>

*Takato Yasuno*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于桥梁损伤原因自动诊断的“损伤原因编码器”，通过结合知识三元组提取、检索增强上下文和QLoRA微调，实现了在消费级硬件上的高效且高精度部署，测试准确率达87.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁基础设施逐渐老化，但盐分侵入、冻结、疲劳开裂等根本原因无法通过肉眼观察，且专家诊断依赖于长期积累的隐性知识。为了自动化这种隐含的因果推理，以支持自主桥梁诊断智能体，作者提出了自动化损伤原因分类的方法。

Method: 方法包含三个串联组件：(i) 知识三元组提取：利用大语言模型从15–35份诊断PDF手册中提取形如“损伤→由引起→原因”的因果三元组，并在FAISS向量存储中索引；(ii) 检索增强上下文：在训练和推理时检索相关因果三元组，与可见损伤描述拼接，将隐性领域知识转化为显式的编码器上下文；(iii) 系统性微调对比：在固定金标准测试集（116个分层样本）上严格比较LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA，证明QLoRA在精度、推理速度和显存占用间达到最佳平衡。

Result: QLoRA实现了与全精度LoRA相同的87.07%测试准确率，同时推理速度提升11%、GPU显存降低72%，在多样化评估中比LoRA高出13个百分点。作者还引入了一个分层、去重并标注难度的金标准测试集作为可复用基准。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在消费级硬件上实现高效、高精度的桥梁诊断智能体，支持边缘部署，为基础设施自动诊断提供了可复现的基准和实用的技术方案。

Abstract: Bridge infrastructure deteriorates gradually, yet its root causes---salt intrusion, freezing, fatigue cracking, and others---remain invisible to the naked eye. Expert diagnosis relies on tacit knowledge built over years of practice. We address the challenge of automating this latent causal reasoning by proposing a Damage Cause Encoder that classifies 10-class damage causes from visible damage descriptions $S_i$ for use in autonomous bridge diagnostic agents. Our approach chains three components: (i)Knowledge Triple Extraction---a large language model extracts causal triples of the form (damage $\xrightarrow{\mathtt{caused\_by}}$ cause) from 15--35 diagnostic PDF manuals and indexes them in a FAISS vector store; (ii)Retrieval-Augmented Context---at training and inference time, relevant causal triples $\mathcal{C}_i$ are retrieved and concatenated with $S_i$, converting implicit domain knowledge into explicit Encoder context; (iii)Systematic Fine-tuning Comparison---we conduct a rigorous comparison of LoRA, QLoRA, and QA-LoRA on a fixed Golden Testset (116 stratified samples), demonstrating that QLoRA achieves the optimal trade-off: identical test accuracy (87.07%) to full-precision LoRA, 11% faster inference, 72% lower GPU memory, and superior generalization across diverse unseen inputs. A controlled Golden Testset---stratified, deduplicated, and difficulty-tagged---is introduced as a reusable benchmark contribution. QLoRA further outperforms LoRA by 13 percentage points on a 100-sample diverse evaluation spanning all 10 damage cause classes.These findings enable memory-efficient, high-accuracy diagnostic agents on consumer-grade hardware for edge deployment.

</details>

*Awsaf Tausif Adib,Md. Shahria Sarker Shuvo,Md. Estehaar Ahmed Emon,Mustafa Kamal,Fuad Rahman,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测中准确建模跨变量依赖关系仍是关键挑战。现有基于注意力机制的方法存在二次复杂度且随变量数量增加扩展性差；近期的无注意力聚合模型虽通过线性复杂度的核心交互解决了复杂度问题，但未能显式利用数据中的全局周期结构。

Method: 提出CARNet（Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution）框架，通过多头核心聚合机制将全局循环周期信息集成到高效的核心交互建模中，实现对跨变量依赖关系的线性复杂度建模。

Result: 在多个真实世界多变量预测基准上进行的广泛实验表明，CARNet在不同预测时域上均持续优于强大的transformer和无注意力基线方法，同时保持了跨变量依赖关系的线性复杂度建模能力。

Conclusion: CARNet成功地将全局周期结构整合到线性复杂度的核心交互建模中，在多变量时间序列预测任务中实现了准确性和效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Accurately modeling cross-variate dependencies remains a key challenge in multivariate time series forecasting, particularly in the presence of strong periodic patterns. Many existing approaches rely on attention-based mechanisms that incur quadratic complexity and scale poorly with increasing numbers of variates. Recent attention-free aggregation models address this issue through linear-complexity core-based interactions, but they do not explicitly leverage the global periodic structure present in the data. To overcome this limitation, we propose CARNet, a Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution framework that integrates global recurrent cycle information into efficient core based interaction modeling via Multihead Core Aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CARNet consistently outperforms strong transformer and non-attention baselines across diverse prediction horizons while preserving linear-complexity modeling of cross-variate dependencies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稀疏注意力方法通过蒸馏教师模型的注意力模式来训练选择器，其核心假设是注意力模式能够揭示教师模型实际使用的上下文内容。然而这一假设缺乏验证：注意力模式可能无法准确反映模型生成答案时真正依赖的证据，导致蒸馏得到的选择器性能受限。

Method: 1. 在证据已知的检索任务上，通过掩码部分上下文并检测答案是否变化来识别模型真正的因果依赖；
2. 对比注意力模式与因果依赖证据的差异；
3. 分别基于注意力模式和因果依赖证据训练选择器并比较性能；
4. 在预训练模型（Qwen2.5-3B、Gemma-2-9B）上验证注意力与依赖的冲突。

Result: 1. 注意力与因果依赖常不一致：教师模型会关注已学会忽略的过时事实，且不同训练轮次间注意力模式变化大但依赖的证据相同；
2. 两步引用任务中，基于注意力训练的选择器准确率仅41%，而基于因果依赖的选择器达99%；
3. 因果依赖证据无需人工标注，仅通过掩码即可从冻结的教师模型中恢复；
4. Qwen2.5-3B在58%冲突事实示例中更关注过时事实而非当前事实；
5. Gemma-2-9B在限定到两句相关文本时准确率从56%升至99%。

Conclusion: 注意力展示的是模型"看"的位置，而非答案所依赖的内容。在测试场景中，因果依赖作为训练目标匹配或优于注意力模式，应重新审视基于注意力蒸馏的稀疏注意力训练范式。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long contexts by allowing each query to read only selected parts of the input. These selectors are often trained by distilling the attention patterns of a dense teacher, assuming that attention reveals which context the teacher actually uses. We test that assumption on retrieval tasks where the evidence for each answer is known exactly. By masking parts of the context and measuring whether the answer changes, we find that attention and causal dependence often disagree, and distilled selectors inherit the mismatch. Teachers attend to outdated facts they have learned to ignore, and their attention can vary across training runs even when they rely on the same evidence. In a two-step reference task, attention at the answer skips the intermediate step because it was resolved earlier in the forward pass: a selector trained on attention achieves 41% accuracy, while the same selector trained on causal evidence reaches 99% and matches the teacher. These evidence sets require no annotation: recovered from a frozen teacher by masking alone, they train selectors to the same accuracy. We find the same conflict in pretrained models: Qwen2.5-3B gives more attention to an outdated fact than the current one on 58% of conflicting-fact examples despite answering correctly, while Gemma-2-9B rises from 56% to 99% accuracy when restricted to the two relevant sentences. Attention shows where a model looks, not necessarily what its answer depends on; across the regimes we tested, that dependence matched or outperformed attention as a training target.

</details>
