Existing skills overemphasize procedural guidance the stage least often responsible for failure while under supporting grounding and verification, the dominant sources of remaining errors. After correcting evaluation artifacts and studying traces, we find many regressions and persistent failures recoverable through better grounding and verification. Procedural skills should be evaluated by decomposing their net effect into gains and regressions, not by aggregate improvement alone. We identify three regression modes skills should avoid, and find that reliability depends more on grounding and verification than on procedural skill choice.

</details>


### [134] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning for assisting Air Traffic Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22525)
*Anduel Mehmeti,Gabriella Gigante,Salvatore Venticinque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在空管(ATC)这一安全关键领域中将可解释性技术应用于强化学习(RL)算法，通过显著图方法揭示了智能体在避开禁飞区的路径决策过程中的关键输入特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在医疗、自动驾驶和航空等高风险关键环境中有效集成AI，并实现更高水平的自动化和无障碍的人机协作，建立对AI解决方案的信任至关重要。而信任与AI系统的可解释性密切相关，尤其是在深度学习应用中，AI可解释性引起了越来越多的关注。

Method: 使用简化的空管环境作为初始测试平台，采用强化学习算法训练智能体，使其能够做出避开禁飞区的替代飞行路线决策。作为初步的可解释性方法，研究采用了显著图技术，该技术能够提供对智能体决策过程中最具影响力的输入特征的洞察。

Result: 研究成功在简化的空管环境中训练出能够做出替代飞行路线决策的强化学习智能体，并通过显著图方法初步实现了对智能体决策过程的可解释性分析，识别出影响决策的关键输入特征。

Conclusion: 本研究为安全关键领域（特别是空管）中强化学习算法的可解释性提供了初步探索和可行方法，为建立对AI系统的信任奠定了基础，有助于推动AI在高风险环境中的有效集成。

Abstract: To effectively integrate AI into high-stakes, critical environments such as healthcare, autonomous driving, and aviation--and to advance toward higher levels of automation and seamless human-AI collaboration--building trust in AI-driven solutions is essential. Trust, in turn, is closely linked to the explainability of AI systems. The rapid advancements in AI across various domains have underscored the challenges of establishing trust, raising increasing interest in AI explainability even more when applied to deep learning. In this context, the present work aims to explore the application of explainability techniques to Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms, specifically within the safety-critical domain of Air Traffic Control (ATC). Using a simplified ATC environment as an initial testbed, an intelligent agent is trained with a reinforcement learning algorithm to make decisions on alternative flight routes that avoid no-fly zones. As a preliminary explainability approach, a saliency map is employed, providing insights into the input features that most significantly influence the agent's decision-making process.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [HEMERA: A Heterogeneous Memory-Centric Accelerator with Recursive Dataflow for Edge-Constrained State-Space-Duality Models Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22022)
*Hao Ding,Ling Liang,Ruitong Qiao,Dongxue Zhao,Xiantong Qiu,Jinshan Li,Meng Li,Lei Jin,Zhiliang Xia,Zongliang Huo,Zongwei Wang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HEMERA加速器，通过将矩阵形式的SSD计算重构为流式递归数据流，在Mamba-2推理中实现1.4x-3.6x延迟加速和12.2x-27.0x能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化状态空间模型（如Mamba）通过SSD实现高效长序列建模，但SSD引入显著的系统级开销，包括二次中间存储、不规则数据移动和前缀依赖执行，导致传统架构上内存流量和带宽需求过高。现有加速器仍保留矩阵导向的SSD执行，无法同时避免二次中间存储并高效映射依赖约束的状态传播。

Method: 提出HEMERA异构内存中心加速器，将矩阵形式SSD计算重构为代数等价的流式递归数据流，避免二次中间存储同时保持原始计算。采用异构执行范式：将密集线性操作映射到存内计算单元，将递归状态更新映射到专用流式引擎。

Result: 在130M至2.8B参数的Mamba-2模型上，相比NVIDIA A100上的官方优化融合Mamba-2内核，HEMERA实现平均1.4x-3.6x延迟加速和12.2x-27.0x能效提升。在长序列推理中将平均SSD相关执行时间比例降至14.12%。

Conclusion: HEMERA通过流式递归数据流重构成功解决了SSD的系统级开销问题，实现了显著的性能和能效提升，展示了在边缘约束下高效部署Mamba-2模型的潜力。

Abstract: Structured State Space Models (SSMs), such as Mamba, enable efficient long-sequence modeling with linear time complexity. Recent implementations realize this capability through Structured State Space Duality (SSD), which transforms recursive state evolution into matrix-form computations. However, SSD introduces substantial system-level overheads, including quadratic intermediate materialization, irregular data movement, and prefix-dependent execution, leading to excessive memory traffic and bandwidth demand on conventional architectures. Although prior accelerators mitigate these overheads through optimized dataflows or compute-in-memory techniques, they largely retain matrix-oriented SSD execution and cannot simultaneously avoid quadratic intermediate storage and efficiently map dependency-bound state propagation.
  This paper presents HEMERA, a heterogeneous memory-centric accelerator for efficient Mamba-2 inference. Rather than directly executing the matrix-form SSD computation, HEMERA reformulates it into an algebraically equivalent streaming-recursive dataflow that avoids quadratic intermediate storage while preserving the original computation. The resulting heterogeneous execution paradigm maps dense linear operations onto in-memory computing units and recursive state updates onto a dedicated streaming engine. Across Mamba-2 models ranging from 130M to 2.8B, HEMERA achieves average latency speedups of 1.4x-3.6x and energy-efficiency improvements of 12.2x-27.0x over the official optimized fused Mamba-2 kernel on NVIDIA A100. It further reduces the average SSD-related execution-time ratio across model scales to 14.12% during long-sequence inference, demonstrating its potential for efficient deployment under edge constraints.

</details>


### [136] [Sparse by Command: Task-Conditional Compute Skipping for Multi-Task Inference Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22038)
*Afzal Ahmad,Gaoyu Mao,Shoubo Hu,Hui-Ling Zhen,Mingxuan Yuan,Xinyu Chen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种硬件/软件协同设计的方法，通过任务条件化的稀疏执行来减少多任务推理模型的计算开销，在CARLA自动驾驶模拟器中实现了66-76%的FLOPs减少和51-59%的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务推理模型在所有任务中共享单一骨干网络，但在推理过程中无论激活哪个任务都执行相同的计算，导致在任务无关操作上浪费大量能量和计算周期。作者观察到任务命令在推理开始前就可获得，这提供了一个免费信号可以在硬件层面跳过不必要的计算。

Method: 采用硬件/软件协同设计方法：1）训练一个轻量级门控网络，与骨干网络联合训练，预测每个tile的二进制执行掩码；2）设计指令集架构，其指令携带每个tile的位掩码字段；3）开发支持稀疏tile执行的平铺推理加速器，具有可配置并行度、双缓冲内存和INT8数据路径。在AMD/Xilinx Alveo U50 FPGA上实现原型系统。

Result: 在CARLA自动驾驶模拟器的闭环视觉运动驾驶任务中，任务条件化稀疏性将FLOPs减少66-76%同时保持驾驶质量；设备上延迟降低51-59%，从9.12ms降至3.74-4.44ms（2.1-2.4倍加速）；每次推理的能耗从263mJ降至108-128mJ。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了任务依赖的计算减少，每个命令仅激活网络中所需的子集，无需更改模型架构或推理流程，为多任务推理系统提供了显著的效率和性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-task inference models share a single backbone across diverse tasks, yet execute identical computation regardless of which task is active - wasting energy and cycles on task-irrelevant operations. We observe that the task command, typically available before inference begins, provides a free signal that can be exploited to skip unnecessary computation at the hardware level. We present a HW/SW co-designed approach in which a lightweight gating network, trained jointly with the backbone, predicts per-tile binary execution masks conditioned on the task input. Each tile corresponds to a fixed group of output channels (the native scheduling granularity of the accelerator), enabling masked tiles to be skipped with zero overhead. This yields a task-dependent reduction in compute, where each command activates only the subset of the network it requires, without changes to the model architecture or inference pipeline.
  We co-design the full system stack: a command-conditioned training procedure that learns hardware-aligned tile masks under a sparsity objective; an instruction set architecture whose instructions carry per-tile bitmask fields, allowing the hardware to skip masked tiles without software intervention; and a tiled inference accelerator with configurable parallelism, double-buffered memory, and INT8 datapath that natively supports sparse tile execution.
  We prototype on an AMD/Xilinx Alveo U50 FPGA and evaluate on a closed-loop visuomotor driving task in CARLA autonomous driving simulator. Task-conditional sparsity reduces FLOPs by 66-76% while maintaining driving quality. On-device latency decreases by 51-59%, from 9.12 ms to 3.74-4.44 ms (2.1-2.4x speedup), with energy per inference dropping from 263 to 108-128mJ.

</details>


### [137] [HiKV: Hierarchical Importance-Aware KV Cache with Hardware Acceleration for LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22389)
*Chao Fang,Jun Yin,Man Shi,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HiKV，一种针对长上下文LLM中KV cache内存瓶颈的算法-硬件协同设计方案，通过分层重要性感知实现KV cache压缩，在精度损失小于1%的情况下达到最高7.95倍加速和90%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型（LLM）在解码过程中，KV cache持续增长已成为关键的内存瓶颈，亟需有效的压缩和加速方案来提升系统效率。

Method: HiKV采用算法-硬件协同设计：算法层面实施两阶段压缩——第一阶段在固定预算内驱逐不重要token，第二阶段仅加载保留token的重要元素；架构层面设计了专用加速器，核心为可重构重要性排序器，在单一电路中以最小开销统一两阶段加速路径。

Result: 在代表性LLM上，相比原始KV cache基线，HiKV在精度损失小于1%条件下实现最高7.95倍加速和90%注意力计算能耗降低；在等精度约束下，相比现有最优重要性方法，外部存储访问减少1.82~4.87倍；专用硬件组件仅增加8%系统面积。

Conclusion: HiKV通过分层重要性感知的算法-硬件协同设计，有效解决了长上下文LLM的KV cache内存瓶颈，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了性能和能效，为长上下文LLM部署提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid adoption of long-context large language models (LLMs), the continuously growing KV cache during decoding has become the critical memory bottleneck. To tackle this challenge, we propose HiKV, a novel algorithm-hardware co-design that exploits KV cache redundancy through hierarchical importance awareness. Algorithmically, HiKV compresses the KV cache at two granularities: Stage I evicts unimportant tokens within a fixed budget, and Stage II further loads only the significant elements of each retained token, reaching compression ratios unattainable at a single granularity. Architecturally, we develop a dedicated accelerator centered on a reconfigurable importance sorter that switches between the distinct sorting datapaths each stage requires, unifying the two-stage acceleration in one circuit with minimal overhead. Evaluated on representative LLMs, HiKV achieves up to 7.95x speedup and 90% energy reduction in the attention computation over the vanilla KV cache baseline within negligible 1% accuracy loss. Under iso-accuracy constraints, HiKV outperforms state-of-the-art importance-based methods by achieving an additional 1.82~4.87x reduction in external memory accesses. These benefits are enabled by specialized hardware components that add only 8% to the system area.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Reliability-Contagion Feasibility in LLM Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21912)
*Ruiwu Niu,Xincheng Shu,Ying Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型代理通信网络中错误信息传播与纠正机制，发现网络连通性需满足可靠性要求与错误控制之间的权衡约束，为在显式可靠性约束下选择网络拓扑提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信使大语言模型代理能够共享证据，但也为错误声明的传播提供了路径。在网络化代理系统中，如何在保证系统可靠性的同时控制错误传播，是一个重要但尚未充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出了一种感知纠正的网络传播模型，追踪易感、暴露、感染和纠正状态的代理，推导异构通信网络的早期入侵条件；将传播模型与分析性多数投票基准耦合；在固定每条边暴露量和固定发送者预算两种设定下分析图约束；通过21,000条轨迹的有限网络模拟和grok-4.3控制实验验证理论预测。

Result: 在固定边暴露设定下，可靠性与错误控制施加相反的图约束，存在使两者交集为空或包含中间连通性范围的情况；识别出可靠图类中具有最小入侵因子的正则图。在固定发送者预算下，同质一阶阈值与网络密度无关，即通信预算设定决定了增加边是否会增加早期传播风险。实验验证了随着度数从2增至4和5，平均首代后代从0.667增至1.333和1.667，暴露邻居的采用率保持0.333。

Conclusion: 研究结果为在显式可靠性和传播约束下选择连通性提供了可处理的理论基础，有助于指导大语言模型代理网络的拓扑设计。

Abstract: Communication allows large language model agents to pool evidence, but it also creates paths along which an erroneous claim can spread. We formulate a correction-aware network model that tracks susceptible, exposed, infectious, and corrected agents and derive its early-invasion condition for heterogeneous communication networks. We then couple this propagation model to an analytic majority-vote benchmark in which a clean-task reliability target imposes a minimum connectivity requirement. Under fixed exposure per communication edge, reliability and error control impose opposing graph constraints. We characterize when their intersection is empty and when it contains an intermediate connectivity range, and identify regular graphs that attain the smallest invasion factor within the reliable graph class when such graphs exist. Under a fixed sender budget, the homogeneous first-order threshold is independent of network density, showing that the communication-budget convention determines whether added edges increase early propagation risk. Finite-network simulations on 21,000 trajectories illustrate these directional predictions. A controlled grok-4.3 experiment then evaluates three six-node topologies on 36 new closed-world tasks, with a balanced 12-task subset continued to full cascades. Mean first-generation offspring increased from 0.667 to 1.333 and 1.667 as degree increased from 2 to 4 and 5, while the adoption fraction among exposed neighbours remained 0.333. Mean non-seed erroneous adoption in the full-cascade subset was 0.200, 0.333, and 0.333. Together, these results provide a tractable basis for selecting connectivity under explicit reliability and propagation constraints.

</details>


### [139] [When Language Models Meet NeuroGraphs: Exploring Enhanced Agentic LLM Framework Towards Brain Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22082)
*Jiaxing Li,Rui Dong,Muyao Tang,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了BrainAgent，一个基于智能体的大语言模型框架，用于知识增强的脑网络分析，将连接组分类重构为迭代式的拓扑理解、知识检索、推理和反思过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习方法主要将连接组分析视为图到逻辑的分类问题，解释性推理能力有限；直接应用通用大语言模型存在结构-语言鸿沟、神经科学基础不足和过度自信预测等挑战。

Method: 将原始脑网络通过脑专用分析工具转换为紧凑的多层级结构描述，检索相关神经科学知识和任务特定案例来支撑推理过程，生成带有反思验证的结构化预测。

Result: 在四个公共rs-fMRI数据集上，BrainAgent在不同闭源和开源LLM骨干网络上均优于直接提示和标准推理基线；消融实验和可解释性分析验证了各组件的有效性，产生了更全面、多层级、可验证的解释。

Conclusion: 智能体大语言模型为可解释、知识基础的脑网络分析提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Brain network analysis is crucial for understanding cognition and neurological disorders, yet existing deep learning methods mainly treat connectome analysis as a graph-to-logit classification problem, offering limited explanatory reasoning. Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising interface for knowledge-intensive scientific analysis, but directly applying general-purpose LLMs to brain networks remains challenging due to the structure-language gap, limited neuroscience grounding, and overconfident positive predictions. In this paper, we propose \textbf{BrainAgent}, an agentic LLM framework for knowledge-enhanced brain network analysis. BrainAgent reformulates connectome classification as an iterative process of topology-aware understanding, external retrieval, reasoning, and reflection. Specifically, it first converts raw brain networks into compact multi-level structural descriptions through brain-specific analysis tools, then retrieves relevant neuroscience knowledge and task-specific cases to ground the reasoning process, and finally generates structured predictions with reflective verification. Experiments on four public rs-fMRI datasets show that BrainAgent consistently improves different closed-source and open-source LLM backbones over direct prompting and standard reasoning baselines. Further ablation and interpretability analyses demonstrate the effectiveness of each component and show that BrainAgent produces more comprehensive, multi-level, and verifiable explanations.These results indicate that agentic LLMs provide a practical route toward interpretable and knowledge-grounded brain network analysis.

</details>


### [140] [Draining the Energy Commons: Self-Defeating Over-Appropriation as a Coordination Failure in Agentic LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22188)
*Marcantonio Bracale Syrnicov,Federico Pierucci,Matteo Prandi,Marcello Galisai,Piercosma Bisconti,Francesco Giarrusso,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM智能体在共享可再生资源时存在协调失败问题。当资源再生率较低时，所有三个模型家族的智能体都会过度消耗共享能源储备，导致自毁式行为模式，类似于急躁的优化者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被部署为规划、使用工具和长期行动的智能体，它们经常共享持久性资源（如计算池或能源储备）。当一个智能体的决策影响后续智能体面临的条件时，可能出现协调失败。研究旨在探索LLM智能体在可再生资源共享环境中的行为模式。

Method: 设计可再生能源公地实验：使用四个同家族的GPT、Gemini或Grok智能体在均匀自博弈中作为电力产消者，指导其最大化运营连续性。保持总剩余需求和决策协议固定，改变共享能源储备的再生率（从丰富到稀缺）。建立两个离线基准：社会规划者最大化群体运营服务价值 vs. 开放获取模式下各自最大化自身价值。

Result: 当需求不超过峰值可再生补充时，三个模型家族都保护储备；但超过该阈值后过度占用（所有九个精确稀缺对比均通过Holm校正，最大调整p=4.87e-5）。在更高稀缺性（rho=1.2）下，早期总请求压力在每个家族中都超过峰值可再生补充，平均为该水平的1.21倍。平均轨迹在第5-7轮低于最大补充的储备水平。实际消耗类似于更急躁的开放获取基准的结果。

Conclusion: LLM智能体表现出系统级对齐失败：保护当前服务的同时损害未来服务，类似于急躁优化者。这种失败模式在隔离响应评估中会被遗漏，凸显了在共享资源环境中评估智能体协调能力的重要性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that plan, use tools, and act over time. When they share persistent resources, such as compute pools or energy reserves, decisions by one agent affect the conditions faced by later agents. We study this coordination failure in a renewable energy commons. Four same-family GPT, Gemini, or Grok agents act in homogeneous self-play as electricity prosumers, instructed to maximize operational continuity. Holding aggregate residual demand and the decision protocol fixed, we vary the regeneration rate of a shared energy reserve from abundance to scarcity. All three families preserve the reserve when demand does not exceed peak renewable replacement, but over-appropriate it beyond that threshold (all nine exact scarcity contrasts survive Holm correction; largest adjusted p = 4.87e-5). The pattern is self-defeating: the same populations protect current service while undermining future service. At higher scarcity (rho = 1.2), early aggregate request pressure exceeds peak renewable replacement in every family and averages 1.21 times that level. Mean trajectories fall below the reserve level of maximum replenishment by rounds 5-7. Two offline benchmarks compare a social planner maximizing group-wide operational-service value with open access, where each prosumer maximizes its own value. At a discount factor of gamma = 0.95, both benchmarks sustain the reserve under the same dynamics. Realized depletion instead resembles outcomes under a more impatient open-access benchmark. The populations therefore behave like impatient optimizers at the level of the public trajectory. This system-level alignment failure would be missed by isolated-response evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [Where FactsGo Missing: A LayerwiseTaxonomy and Per-Layer Attribution of Information Omissionin Air-Gapped LLM Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22448)
*Santhiya Rajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对离线部署场景中4-8B量化模型的遗漏问题（关键事实缺失），提出了9层分类体系（L0-L8），并通过75,476次实验发现68%的遗漏源于确定性中间件层（L0-L3），为运维干预提供了明确指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗FHIR服务、法律审查和主权基础设施等受监管的离线部署场景中，无法调用前沿API，只能运行量化的小型模型。这些系统的核心可靠性问题是遗漏——即关键事实的静默缺失（如代理仅读取400条记录中的20条后报告“无异常”）。作者认为遗漏是流水线现象而非模型现象，需要系统性的分析框架来定位和量化各层的遗漏机制。

Method: 1）构建九层分类体系（L0-L8），覆盖从数据摄取到代理循环的所有遗漏机制；2）提出归因方法论，通过控制消融和对数分解将确定性层（L0-L3）与行为层（L4-L8）分离，用量化遗漏瀑布图量化各层贡献；3）开发跨架构测试框架，比较滑动窗口混合、全注意力和SSM混合模型；4）构建运行时检测框架，用于拥有对数的离线场景。

Result: 在5个模型和2个引擎上进行的75,476次试验显示，汇总遗漏率为0.62。其中68%的遗漏源于确定性中间件层（L0-L3），这重新定位了运维人员应干预的位置。服务器端配置因子（权重量化、KV缓存类型、RoPE缩放）被固定，留待后续研究。

Conclusion: 遗漏问题主要集中在确定性中间件层而非模型本身，这意味着运维干预应聚焦于L0-L3层。该发现为离线部署场景的系统可靠性改进提供了明确的优化方向，降低了对模型能力改进的依赖。

Abstract: Air-gapped and on-premises deployments in regulated settings (clinical FHIR services, legal review, sovereign infrastructure) cannot call frontier APIs; they run quantized 4-8B models via llama.cpp or vLLM behind tool servers. The dominant reliability failure is omission: the silent absence of a decision-critical fact, such as an agent reading 20 of 400 records and reporting "no anomalies." We argue omission is a pipeline phenomenon, not a model phenomenon, and make four contributions. First, a nine-layer taxonomy (L0-L8) locating every omission mechanism from ingestion through the agent loop. Second, an attribution methodology separating deterministic layers (L0-L3) from behavioral layers (L4-L8) via controlled ablation and logit decomposition, quantifying each with an omission waterfall. Third, an open cross-architecture harness comparing sliding-window-hybrid, full-attention, and SSM-hybrid models across engines and frameworks. Fourth, a runtime-detection framework for air-gapped settings where you own the logits. Results from a 75,476-trial sweep across five models and two engines show a pooled omission rate of 0.62; 68% originates in deterministic middleware (L0-L3), relocating where operators should intervene. Server-side profile factors (weight quantization, KV-cache type, RoPE scaling) were fixed and left for future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [MSSI: Middleware for Unified Semantic and Syntactic Interoperability in IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21784)
*Sanku Kumar Roy,Sudip Misra,Narendra Singh Raghuwanshi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向物联网发布-订阅框架的中间件解决方案，通过语法翻译和基于MLP的语义翻译实现异构设备间的统一互操作性，分类准确率达95.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网需求的增长，具有不同语义和数据格式的异构IoT设备之间需要无缝可靠的通信来实现应用过程的自主控制，而互操作性问题是实现这一目标的主要挑战。

Method: 提出了一种中间件框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)新颖的消息语法翻译方法，解决用户与设备间的语法差异；(2)基于多层感知机(MLP)的语义互操作性框架，将设备信息翻译为用户请求的语义；同时提出了特征提取算法来提取原始和判别性特征作为MLP模型的输入。

Result: 在考虑多种公开使用的数据格式和属性语义注释的情况下，MLP模型的整体分类准确率达到95.78%，能够有效确定发布者传入消息中每个属性的标准含义。

Conclusion: 所提出的中间件解决方案能够有效解决物联网网络中的语义和语法互操作性问题，通过统一的数据格式转换和语义翻译，实现了异构IoT设备间的无缝通信，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the growing demand of Internet of Things (IoT), there is a need for seamless and reliable communication between heterogeneous IoT devices and the cyber-world to ensure autonomous control over any application process. More specifically, seamless communication requires interoperability between heterogeneous devices (actors) having different semantics and data formats (syntaxes), while making it more challenging. In this paper, we propose a middleware solution for unified semantic and syntactic interoperability in the publisher-subscriber framework of IoT network. The proposed framework automatically translates the subscribers (users) compatible syntax and semantics of the receiver message from the publishers (IoT devices). First, we propose a novel method of syntax translation of messages, to solve the syntactic disparities between users and devices, while providing the information in the user requested syntax. Thereafter, a multilayer perceptron (MLP)-based semantic interoperability framework is proposed to translate the device information to the user requested semantics. Additionally, a novel algorithm is proposed for extracting raw and discriminative features, which are to be fitted to the MLP model as inputs. To show the effectiveness of the proposed middleware, we evaluate different parameters, while considering various publicly used data formats and semantic annotations of attributes to ensure the versatility of the proposed middleware in the practical scenario. The overall classification accuracy using MLP is $95.78$\% for determining the standard meaning of each attribute of the incoming message from the publisher to address the semantic interoperability problem in IoT.

</details>


### [143] [Fewer Paths, Better Performance: Understanding the ZCube Topology through Braess's Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21893)
*Li Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文挑战数据中心网络的多路径教条，提出ZCube拓扑通过移除Spine层和消除路径多路性，反而为大型语言模型训练和推理提供更好性能，并揭示了其与Braess悖论的结构联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据中心网络遵循多路径教条：在端点间提供多条路径，通过哈希将流分散到各路径，利用冗余吸收故障和负载不均。然而，对于结构化的LLM流量，这种多路径方法存在Braess悖论问题：在拥塞无感知路由条件下，添加路径反而可能损害性能，而移除路径反而可能改善性能。

Method: 1）理论分析：建立多路径网络与Braess悖论的结构联系，证明静态ECMP哈希在最坏情况下允许无界失衡，而贪心路由在仿射延迟下达到最优的4/3倍均衡；2）ZCube设计：移除Spine层，消除路径多路性，采用正交双分区架构；3）负载均衡证明：证明ZCube对Braess悖论免疫，且其正交双分区可证明对任意流量矩阵实现负载均衡；4）权衡分析：用毫秒级控制平面恢复替代微秒级哈希恢复。

Result: 在服务于GLM-5.1编码推理的生产集群中，ZCube实现：网络成本降低33%，GPU吞吐量提高15%，P99首个令牌时间降低40.6%。AllReduce训练和解耦推理中的KV缓存传输作为正交双分区负载均衡的两个推论自然导出。

Conclusion: 对于模型结构驱动的工作负载，将拓扑结构与流量模式匹配比路径多路性更为重要。ZCube通过消除路径多路性反而获得了更好的性能，这表明在特定工作负载下，简单且匹配的结构设计优于复杂的多路径冗余设计。

Abstract: Datacenter networks follow a multipath doctrine: provision many paths between endpoints, hash flows across them, and let redundancy absorb both failures and load imbalance. The ZCube topology violates this doctrine. It removes the Spine layer, eliminates path multiplicity, and cuts one third of switching hardware, yet delivers better performance for both large model training and inference. We explain this anomaly through a structural connection to Braess's paradox, first observed in 1968: both phenomena trace to congestion-oblivious routing over competing paths. Braess showed that adding paths under this condition can hurt; ZCube shows that removing paths under the same condition can help. First, we show that multipath fabrics under structured LLM traffic operate in Braess's shadow: static ECMP hashing is strictly more fragile than greedy routing. Greedy routing reaches an equilibrium within 4/3 of optimal for affine latencies; static hashing admits unbounded imbalance in the worst case. Second, we prove that ZCube is immune to Braess's paradox and that its orthogonal dual partition provably balances load for arbitrary traffic matrices; AllReduce in training and KV cache transfers in disaggregated inference fall out as two corollaries. Third, we quantify the price of this immunity: ZCube trades microsecond hash recovery for millisecond control plane recovery, a trade that upper-layer resilience in LLM serving makes favorable. Production measurements from a cluster serving GLM-5.1 coding inference report 33% lower network cost, 15% higher GPU throughput, and 40.6% lower P99 time to first token. Our analysis suggests that for workloads driven by model structure, matching topology to traffic matters more than path multiplicity.

</details>


### [144] [Location-Aware NAS Timer Optimization in NTN-TN Integrated Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21947)
*Cheng Liu,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对LEO NTN-TN融合网络的5G注册，提出一种位置感知的UE特定NAS定时器优化方法，有效降低注册时延、能耗和无效尝试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3GPP NAS定时器采用固定值，而先前的闭式定时器模型仅在网络层面假设下计算全局定时器，两者均无法反映UE级别的传播时延、AMF到达位置和路径可靠性差异，导致定时器配置不合理，影响5G注册的可靠性和能效。

Method: 提出一种面向LEO NTN-TN融合网络的位置感知、UE特定的NAS定时器优化方法。该方法利用服务链路几何、地面站距离和星间链路（ISL）跳数建模路径时延分量，并根据每个UE的预期AMF队列暴露和路径可靠性自适应调整端点时延分量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与固定定时器和全局定时器配置相比，该方法有效降低了注册时延、UE能耗和可避免的注册尝试次数，尤其在定时器过度配置导致不必要等待的情况下效果显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的UE特定、位置感知的NAS定时器优化方法能够有效解决LEO NTN-TN融合网络中现有定时器机制的局限性，显著提升5G注册性能和能量效率。

Abstract: Efficient Non-Access Stratum (NAS) timer configuration is critical for reliable and energy-efficient Fifth Generation (5G) registration in Non-Terrestrial Network (NTN)-Terrestrial Network (TN) integrated systems, where Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite access introduces large registration bursts, heterogeneous propagation paths, and multi-hop satellite routing. Existing 3GPP NAS timers use fixed values, while prior closed-form timer models compute a global timer under network-level assumptions; both fail to capture user equipment (UE)-level differences in propagation delay, Access and Mobility Management Function (AMF) arrival position, and path reliability. In this paper, we propose a location-aware, UE-specific NAS timer optimization method for LEO NTN-TN integrated networks. The proposed method models the path-delay component using service-link geometry, ground-station distance, and Inter-Satellite Link (ISL) hop count, and adapts the endpoint-delay component according to each UE's expected AMF queue exposure and path reliability. Simulation results show that our method reduces registration latency, UE energy consumption, and avoidable registration attempts compared with fixed and global timer configurations, especially when timer over-provisioning causes unnecessary waiting.

</details>


### [145] [Predictive Lightweight MARL for Resilient Coverage in Sparse-Signaling Aerial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22109)
*Chuan-Chi Lai,Ang-Hsun Tsai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了预测式轻量多智能体强化学习（PL-MARL）框架，通过运动学感知推理引擎在通信受限的无人机集群中实现韧性覆盖，有效解决了信号稀疏和信息老化导致的协调崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽受限的无人机集群在信号稀疏和信息老化情况下容易出现协调崩溃，传统的协调方法需要频繁通信，但通信资源稀缺且需要为载荷服务预留频谱，因此需要一种能在极低通信开销下保持协调韧性的方法。

Method: 提出预测式轻量多智能体强化学习（PL-MARL）框架，引入运动学感知推理引擎，利用物理先验知识主动重建邻居轨迹，实现计算与通信的高效权衡，将结构韧性与信号频率解耦。

Result: 仿真实验表明，PL-MARL在极端信号稀缺和节点失效情况下能够保持优越的覆盖性能和任务连续性，验证了主动推理作为可扩展、低延迟解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: PL-MARL框架成功实现了在保持协调韧性的同时最小化控制开销，为载荷服务保留了频谱资源，同时确保了对干扰的韧性，为鲁棒的空中协调提供了可扩展的低延迟解决方案。

Abstract: This letter proposes the Predictive Lightweight Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (PL-MARL) framework to ensure resilient coverage in bandwidth-constrained UAV swarms. To counter coordination collapse caused by sparse signaling and information aging, we introduce a Kinematic-Aware Inference Engine that proactively reconstructs neighbor trajectories via physical priors. This approach enables an efficient computation-for-communication trade-off, decoupling structural resilience from signaling frequency. Simulations confirm that PL-MARL maintains superior coverage and mission continuity under extreme signaling scarcity and node failure. Our results validate proactive inference as a scalable, low-latency solution for robust aerial coordination, effectively minimizing control overhead to preserve spectrum for payload services while ensuring resilience against interference.

</details>


### [146] [A Self-Calibrating Agentic AI Framework for Autonomous Edge Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22400)
*Fin Gentzen,Marla Grunewald,Iulisloi Zacarias,Mounir Bensalem,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自校准的智能体AI框架，通过集成ARIMA预测器来缓解操作漂移并动态逼近真实值，在零知识工作负载资源使用预测任务中显著提升了预测准确性和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被部署为自主智能体，其操作可靠性面临两大根本挑战：开放环境中缺乏可靠的真实值（ground truth），以及随着时间推移出现操作漂移（operational drift）的风险。

Method: 提出了一种自校准机制，通过集成ARIMA预测器来缓解漂移并动态逼近真实值，实现无需持续人工监督的自主完整性保障，并应用于边缘计算网络中零知识工作负载的资源使用建模任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该自校准智能体框架在零知识工作负载资源使用预测上，相比基线LLM智能体预测准确率提高了91.7%，相比纯分析方法预测速度提高了71.7%；所提出的ARIMA跳跃算法生成真实值的速度比标准ARIMA算法快52%，同时保持相同的准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个坚实的框架基础，能够有效保障LLM驱动系统在去中心化基础设施中部署的自主完整性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, transitioning from static conversational interfaces to dynamic systems capable of complex reasoning, tool execution, and decision-making. However, the operational reliability of these agentic AI systems is fundamentally challenged by the absence of reliable ground truth in open-ended environments and the risk of increasing operational drift over time. To address this challenge, we propose and experimentally evaluate an agentic AI framework, designed to enforce autonomous integrity within LLM-driven systems. We design a self-calibration mechanism that mitigates drift and dynamically approximates ground truth by incorporating an ARIMA forecaster, without requiring continuous human oversight. To demonstrate the effectiveness and reliability of our methodology, we apply it to the complex domain of profiling the resource usage of zero-knowledge workloads in edge computing networks. Experimental results show that the proposed self-calibrating agentic framework successfully profiles the zero-knowledge workloads, achieving a higher accuracy than baseline LLM agents by 91.7% for resource usage prediction and improving the prediction speed by 71.7% compared to pure profiling, establishing a robust foundation for deploying autonomous AI in decentralized infrastructures. Furthermore, the ground truth generation using the proposed ARIMA leaping algorithm is 52% faster than a standard ARIMA forecasting algorithm, while achieving the same accuracy.
